下次發布日期：115 年 12 月 11 日下午 4 時

115年 10月賦稅收入初步統計 
一、整體賦稅收入 
（一）實徵淨額 
10 月實徵淨額 5,669 億元，較上年同月增加 1,495 億元；累計 1 至
10 月實徵 4 兆 1,383 億元，較上年同期增加 8,499 億元(+25.8%)，
主要由證交稅、營所稅及營業稅所貢獻。隨新版財政收支劃分法實
施，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率由 61.2%降為 4.5%(獲配稅額隨之減
少 2,662 億元)，致中央政府稅收增加數 3,906 億元(+15.8%)，不及
地方政府增加數 4,671 億元(+68.5%)。 
（二）預算達成率 
累 計1 至 10 月 實 徵 淨 額 占 全 年 預 算 數 91.4% ， 其 中 中 央 政 府 為
92.8%，地方政府為87.7%；與全年預算數差距分別為3,913億元、
2,227億元、1,614億元。 
圖 1 賦稅實徵淨額及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）10 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營利事業所得稅增加 1,133 億元、
證券交易稅增加 220 億元及綜合所得稅增加 120 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,297
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 2,986 億元及營業稅增加 1,272 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-10 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)10 月實徵淨額 4,144 億元，較上年同月增加 1,133 億元(+37.6%)，
主因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 1 兆 4,172 億元，較上年同期增加 2,986
億元(+26.7%)，主因結算申報自繳、暫繳稅款俱增及延分期稅款
陸續入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
10 月實徵淨額 614 億元，較上年同月增加 120 億元(+24.3%)，主因
扣繳稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 8,894 億元，較上年同
期增加 910 億元(+11.4%)，主因結算申報自繳稅額增加，以及盈餘分

配、薪資所得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
(1)10月受進口代徵稅額增加及適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅增加
之交互影響，營業稅實徵淨額為負77億元，較上年同月(負146億元)
增加69億元。
(2)累 計1 至10 月 實 徵 淨額6,153 億 元 ， 較 上年 同 期 增 加1,272 億元
(+26.1%)，主因內需穩定，資通與視聽產品、電子零組件等進口
代徵稅額增加，惟外銷營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

3
4.貨物稅
10月實徵淨額73億元，較上年同月減少41億元(-35.6%)，累計1至10
月實徵淨額948億元，較上年同期減少248億元(-20.7%)，主因新購汽
機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增加，加以汽柴油減徵幅度擴大所致。
5.證券交易稅
(1)10 月及累計 1 至 10 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 1,210
億元、1 兆 1,437 億元，分別較上年同期(6,267 億元、4,632 億元)
增 78.9%、1.5 倍。
(2)10 月證交稅實徵淨額 523 億元， 較上年同月增加 220 億元
(+72.6%)；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 5,583 億元，較上年同期增加
3,297 億元(+1.4 倍)。
6.關稅
10 月實徵淨額 132 億元，較上年同月增加 7 億元(+6.0%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 1,356 億元，較上年同期增加 88 億元(+6.9%)，均因機
械及電機設備、基本金屬、塑橡膠及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
10 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月減少 11 億元(-31.9%)，主因大額

稅款案件減少；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 234 億元，較上年同期增加
32 億元(+15.9%)，主因大額稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
10 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+5.9%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 560 億元，較上年同期減少
11 億元(-2.0%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.使用牌照稅
10 月實徵淨額 6 億元，較上年同月減少 1 億元(-10.2%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 676 億元，較上年同期減少 1 億元(-0.1%)，主因開徵
輛數減少，以及稅款入帳時間差異所致。
（三）前 10月主要稅目實徵淨額占全年預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%(已提前達成全年預算目標)者有遺產稅(123%)、贈與稅
(107%)、印花稅(106%)、房屋稅(105%)及營所稅(102%)。
2.低於 100%者有牌照稅(99%)、營業稅(94%)、綜所稅(93%)、契稅
(90%)、關稅(88%)、菸酒稅(81%)、證交稅(80%)、貨物稅(79%)、土

增稅(71%)及特銷稅(47%)等。

三、政府別賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 10 月中央政府賦稅收入(不含中央統籌分配稅款)實徵淨
額 2 兆 8,579 億元(占全年預算數 92.8%)，較上年同期增加 3,906 億元
(+15.8%)，主因新版財政收支劃分法施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率
大幅調降，獲配稅額隨之減少 2,662 億元，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加
3,297 億元、3,306 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 10 月地方政府賦稅
收入(含中央統籌分配稅款)實
徵淨額 1 兆 1,487 億元(占全年
預算數 87.7%)，較上年同期增
加 4,671 億元(+68.5%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配之營業稅
增加 4,013 億元所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 10 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 220 億元，較
上年同期增加 4 億元(+1.7%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 144
億元，較上年同期減少 6 億元(-4.0%)；撥入長照基金 953 億元(其中 575

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、220 億元來自菸稅，158 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.2%)。

## Document 041: 20261102_投資週報_美股財報表現依然強勁，但現金流疑慮持續增溫

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261102_投資週報_美股財報表現依然強勁，但現金流疑慮持續增溫.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 02 日
上週重點訊息
◼ Fed 維持利率不變，兩位委員主張升息：美國聯準會於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 的表決結果，
將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在 3.75%至 4%，其中理事庫克與克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪
克對該決議投下反對票，並主張應升息 1 碼。Fed 在聲明中表示，經濟活動正以強勁步調擴
張，但通膨仍然偏高。在已有兩位委員主張升息的情況下，後續通膨數據將是 Fed 12 月是
否升息的重要影響因素。
◼ Alphabet 自由現金流轉負幅度超出預期：Alphabet 於 10 月 28 日公布第三季財報，自由現

金流為負 90.7 億美元，明顯低於分析師預期的負 27 億美元，同時 Alphabet 維持全年
1,950 億至 2,050 億美元的資本支出指引，顯示 AI 基礎建設投資仍持續消耗公司現金。
Alphabet 該季自由現金流明顯轉負，也引發市場對於科技巨頭資本支出的疑慮，並帶動整體
科技類股出現修正。
◼ 英特爾因 PC 需求疲弱下修第四季財測：英特爾於 10 月 29 日公布第三季財報，營收年增
21%至 165.1 億美元，高於公司原先財測；其中資料中心與 AI 部門營收年增 78%，為 15 年
來最強勁的成長，但以 PC 為主的客戶端運算部門營收則年減 5%。公司指出，由於記憶體短
缺推升 PC 售價，加上企業 IT 預算受限，使 PC 需求轉弱。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：本次財報季顯示雲端營收加速成
長與未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求仍強；但部分企業的資本支出攀升、自由現金流轉弱，加
上 Fed 可能於 12 月升息，短線科技股波動恐將延續。然而中期而言，AI 產業將由全面成長

轉向基本面分化，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健且 AI 投資已能轉化為獲利的企業，風險報酬相
對有利；對高度依賴外部融資、自由現金流持續為負的企業則宜維持審慎。
◼ 5 至 10 年期美債及投資等級公司債兼顧票息收益與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府
與企業發債供給維持高檔，可能使市場利率中期仍處於較高水準，但近期公債殖利率回落，
顯示市場避險需求升溫，支撐債券買盤；整體而言，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投
資風險，長天期債券則較容易受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，5-10 年期美國
公債與投資級債券則較能兼顧息收與利率風險。

多重利空夾擊，全球 AI 股連日重挫
2026 年 11 月 20 日
一、市場動態
1. 美股 11/19 反彈遭逢賣壓：Anthropic 19 日於那斯達克掛牌，募資約 813 億美元創史
上最大 IPO，開盤大漲 34%、盤中市值一度突破 2 兆美元，終場漲幅收斂至 17.1%，
然激勵效果未能擴散至其他 AI 股，反被視為「利多出盡」的獲利了結訊號。美股四大
指數開高走低、全數重挫逾 2%：道瓊下跌 2.19%，為 6 月以來首度跌破 50,000 點；
標普 500 跌 2.02%、連六黑，逾 460 檔成分股收黑；那斯達克跌 2.25%、費半跌 2.41%，

VIX 盤中一度升近 29、創 3 月以來新高。個股方面，輝達早盤一度上漲 4.7%後翻黑，
收跌 3.3%，本月累計跌幅約 28%；AMD 遭放空機構 Hunterbrook 指控與受美國制裁
的中國伺服器業者往來，重挫 7.72%，高度依賴舉債擴張的 CoreWeave、Nebius 分
別重挫 6.1%與 9.4%。Fed 官員密集釋出鷹派訊號，警告 AI 建設熱潮恐推升通膨，市
場預估 12 月升息機率約 96%；初領失業金人數亦高於預期，增添景氣疑慮。
2. 亞股 11/20 續挫，台股同步承壓：亞股 11/20 早盤延續美股走勢，賣壓擴及亞洲 AI
硬體供應鏈，日經 225 與韓國 KOSPI 同步重挫逾 4%。台股 19 日下跌 1,768.02 點，
收 38,426.89 點，跌幅 4.40%，創史上收盤第 7 大跌點後，20 日再受美股下挫拖累，
加權指數持續走低，截至台灣時間下午 1 點 22 分為止，台股加權指數下挫 6.80%；台
積電、聯發科、台達電等權值股同步重挫。

−2,611.92 −6.80% 美國對中管制升級、AI 投報疑慮、升息壓力夾擊
資料來源：臺灣證券交易所、元大銀行整理；11/20 為盤中數據，台灣時間 11/20 上午 11:50
二、下跌原因
1. 美國對中 AI 管制全面升級，從「晶片」延伸到「算力」與「代工」：美國商務部工業暨
安全局（BIS）於 11 月 16 日發布臨時最終規則、並於 19 日生效，將管制由實體晶片

擴大至「提供雲端算力給中國客戶的第三國資料中心」，並將印尼、馬來西亞、新加坡、
泰國及巴西列為高轉運風險國家，同時限縮晶圓代工與封測廠的許可豁免。此次新規
由財政部與白宮國安會主導起草，反映黃仁勳辭任後政策方向明顯從嚴。對台股而言，

3. 輝達「利多不漲」且接班未定，市場由交易成長轉為交易風險：輝達 FY2027 第三季營

4. Anthropic 史上最大 IPO 引發資金排擠與「利多出盡」情緒：Anthropic 募資約 813
億美元，盤中市值一度突破 2 兆美元，但美股開高走低、晶片股由紅翻黑，市場將其視
為 AI 交易階段性高點的獲利了結訊號。市場亦解讀，大型 AI 模型公司直接上市，讓資
金可直接押注 AI 應用端，相對降低透過硬體供應鏈間接參與的需求，並在短期內吸納
部分 AI 配置資金。
5. 台股籌碼面賣壓與融資斷頭形成負向循環：外資 11 月累計賣超達 8,303 億元，台指期
淨空單維持約 9.9 萬口；台股跌破年線後融資維持率偏低，斷頭與停損賣壓同步出籠，
新台幣亦貶至 33 元附近，加重外資匯損考量。金管會雖自 20 日起實施限空令 2.0（個

股收跌逾 3.5%者隔日禁止平盤下放空、盤中借券賣出上限由 3%降至 1%），但短線仍
難完全抵銷被動賣壓。
資料來源：臺灣證券交易所、元大銀行整理
三、市場看法
1. AI 需求尚未反轉，但產業正由資本支出驅動轉向獲利驗證：我們認為，判斷 AI 產業是
否出現結構性反轉，關鍵應在於終端運算需求、CSP 資本支出及供應鏈訂單是否持續下

### Page 4

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
降，而非單純觀察股價跌幅。輝達第三季資料中心營收年增 110%，且 Vera Rubin 平
台已進入出貨階段，顯示先進運算設備需求仍具有實質支撐。此外，Reuters 引述 LSEG
資料指出，輝達財報公布後，分析師對下一財年調整後淨利的平均預估由 3,800 億美元
上修至 3,930 億美元，但股價評價反而轉趨保守。這項變化反映市場並未全面否定輝達
的獲利能力，而是開始要求更高的風險補償。我們認為，若未來 CSP 未大幅縮減資本
支出，AI 晶片與相關供應鏈仍具有中長期成長基礎；但若資本支出成長持續快於營收

及現金流改善，科技股評價仍可能承壓。
2. AI 信用風險仍較集中於高槓桿業者，尚未顯示全面性信用危機：目前市場對 AI 企業融
資風險的擔憂逐漸升高，但不同企業的財務承受能力存在明顯差異。大型 CSP 通常擁
有成熟的雲端、廣告或軟體業務，可透過營業現金流支應部分 AI 投資；相較之下，部
分新興資料中心業者高度依賴債務、設備融資或外部股權資金，對利率與資本市場環境
更加敏感。10 月 FOMC 會議紀錄指出，投資級公司債利差僅小幅擴大，且以歷史標準
衡量仍相對偏低；高收益債利差則擴大較明顯，尤其與資料中心融資有關的債券受到更
大衝擊。這顯示市場正在提高部分高風險企業的融資門檻，但尚不足以判定信用壓力已
全面擴散至整體投資級債市場。我們認為，後續仍應觀察相關企業的債務到期結構、再
融資需求與信用利差變化，判斷風險是否進一步擴大。
3. 科技股次產業將進一步分化：隨著市場由追逐 AI 投資規模轉向評估投資效益，科技次
產業未來表現可能明顯分歧。晶圓代工與先進封裝產業具有較高技術門檻，仍可受惠先

進運算晶片需求；HBM 及高階記憶體則具備 AI 伺服器成長題材，但其獲利仍需觀察供
需循環及價格變化；半導體設備業者則較容易受到客戶建廠時程及資本支出調整影響。
另一方面，部分尚未建立穩定營收模式的 AI 基礎建設業者，可能因融資成本與投資回
收壓力，面臨更大的評價修正。我們認為，科技股中期並非全面轉空，而是市場可能更
偏好具備技術競爭優勢、穩定訂單、獲利成長及自由現金流的企業。未來即使 AI 產業
整體維持成長，也不代表所有相關公司的營收、獲利及股價都能同步受惠。
4. Fed 政策仍有不確定性，但企業獲利成長可望部分抵銷評價壓力：目前 Fed 升息預期
已對全球科技股評價形成明顯壓力，但未來市場方向不僅取決於 12 月是否升息，更重
要的是升息幅度與後續政策路徑是否超出市場預期。然而，美國經濟與企業獲利目前尚
未出現全面性衰退跡象，若未來企業營收與獲利持續成長，即使本益比難以回到過去高
檔，仍有助於股市透過基本面改善逐步修復跌幅。我們認為，Fed 政策風險不容忽視，
但也不宜單憑升息預期就認定股市中長期多頭結構已全面改變。

5. 台股短線仍需消化籌碼壓力，但企業獲利成長仍可提供中期支撐：若以台股走勢來看，
其近期跌幅明顯超過部分國際市場，我們認為除受到美股科技股修正影響，也與大型電
子權值股集中、外資調節及短線市場流動性下降有關。不過，根據金管會公布數據，截
至 2026 年 10 月底，國內上市櫃公司累計營收約 56.75 兆元，年增 39.05%；前三季
累計稅前淨利約 6.61 兆元，年增 86.43%，顯示整體企業獲利仍具成長支撐。此外，
金管會自 11 月 20 日起擴大限空措施，對收盤跌幅逾 3.5%的個股實施次日平盤下放空
限制，並將盤中借券賣出數量上限由 3%降至 1%。我們認為，政策措施有助降低部分

### Page 5

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
短線賣壓，但市場真正止穩仍需觀察外資流向、融資去槓桿是否趨緩，以及主要電子權
值股的股價與基本面是否恢復穩定。
6. 後續觀察重點：（1）美國下週公布核心 PCE 物價指數，市場預期年增 3.2%，將影響
12 月升息幅度；（2）12 月 FOMC 升息 1 碼或 2 碼，以及 2027 年升息路徑；（3）輝
達新任執行長人選與黃仁勳赴參議院作證情況，牽動出口管制後續走向；（4）BIS 新規
執行細節及東南亞資料中心許可審查進度。
四、 投資建議
1. 短線嚴控槓桿與部位集中度，選股邏輯轉向「現金流與訂單能見度」優先：我們認為，

AI 產業成長動能尚未確認反轉，但市場已不再接受單純依靠資本支出擴張支撐評價。
前述利空因素短期內恐難完全消除或明朗，市場短線仍將隨著消息面的發展而變化。我
們預期後續投資人將更重視科技股的訂單能見度、企業獲利、資產負債表及自由現金
流。同時，台灣在先進製程、先進封裝及 AI 伺服器領域仍具競爭優勢，中期基本面展
望維持偏正向。然而，近期指數連續重挫，反映市場流動性與籌碼壓力尚未解除，短期
仍須觀察外資賣壓、融資水位及美股科技股走勢。投資人宜採取分批及分散方式參與中
長期產業成長，同時避免過度集中於融資需求高、獲利能見度較低的 AI 企業。
2. 以股債平衡穿越波動，固定收益「先短後長」：Fed 仍處升息循環，長天期債券價格仍
有波動風險，但美國公債目前具有高殖利率吸引力與防禦價值，建議以中短天期美國公
債、高信評投資等級債及美元貨幣型商品鎖定相對高檔的收益，待升息循環接近尾聲再
逐步拉長存續期間。投資級債則受惠相對穩健的信用品質，惟仍須關注 AI 相關企業信
用風險是否進一步擴散。

1 
[Table_ReportInfo] 2026 年 11 月 16 日

数据点评 
新兴产业与外需支撑景气，内需修复持续承压 
——2026 年 10 月经济数据点评

点评

西 南证券研究院 
[Table_Summary]  摘要：工业生产方面，10 月规模以上工业增加值同比增长 4.8%，增速较上月
回落 0.7 个百分点，低于市场预期。高技术制造业和装备制造业增加值分别增
长 17.6%、10.6%，其中高技术制造业创近五年来单月增速新高，AI相关电子
硬件、工业机器人、3D 打印设备等产品产量保持较快增长。同时，10 月规上
工业出口交货值增长 18.6%，外需仍是工业生产的重要支撑。整体工业生产呈
现总量放缓、结构分化的特征，高技术制造和出口链条景气度较高，传统周期
性行业修复相对滞后。固定资产投资方面，1-10 月全国固定资产投资同比下降
8.1%，降幅较 1-9 月扩大 0.9 个百分点，投资需求仍处于深度收缩区间。基建
投资方面，1-10 月广义基建投资下降 5.1%，专项债发行节奏放缓、资金向实
物工作量转化存在时滞，整体托底效应尚未充分显现，但互联网和相关服务业
投资，以及交通运输、电力供应等重点领域投资仍保持正增长。随着专项债剩
余额度加快使用及“两重”项目建设推进，重点基建领域投资有望获得一定支
撑，但扭转整体投资下行趋势仍面临较大难度。房地产投资方面，1-10 月房地
产开发投资同比下降 21.6%，降幅较 1-9 月扩大 1.0 个百分点，地产仍是整体
投资的重要拖累。供给端施工、竣工面积单月同比分别下降 32.5%、39.1%，
降幅均有所扩大，新开工降幅有所收窄但仍处负增长区间；需求端商品房销售
面积、销售额分别下降 20.7%、20.5%，降幅小幅收窄，但销售修复尚未有效
传导至房企投资和施工活动。房企资金来源继续收缩，行业投资意愿和项目建
设规模仍然偏弱，预计年内地产投资仍处深度调整阶段，降幅收窄时点或推迟
至 2027 年。制造业投资方面，1-10 月制造业投资同比下降 2.9%，降幅较 1-9
月扩大 0.8 个百分点，企业资本开支意愿仍然偏弱。高技术制造业投资增长
3.0%，电子电路、锂离子电池和集成电路制造业投资增速较快，科技产业投资
仍具韧性。“两新”政策继续支撑设备更新，设备工器具购置投资增长 8.9%，
占全部投资比重提升至 20.3%。但汽车、通用设备等装备制造行业投资明显走
弱，反映生产端韧性尚未转化为新增资本开支。消费方面，10 月社会消费品
零售总额同比增长 0.4%，增速较上月回落 0.7 个百分点，消费需求整体偏弱。
汽车类零售同比下降 21.4%，仍是社零增长的主要拖累；通讯器材、金银珠宝
等品类受前期高基数及金价波动影响，零售增速明显回落，家电、家具消费则
受低基数影响边际改善。服务消费相对更具韧性，1-10 月服务零售额增长
4.9%，高于商品零售额 4.0 个百分点。往后看，汽车及地产相关消费能否改善、
以旧换新政策效应能否延续，仍是影响商品消费修复的重要因素，服务消费则
有望继续成为居民消费的重要支撑。 
 工业生产总量边际降温，科技制造与出口链条维持高景气。2026 年 10 月，全
国规模以上工业增加值同比增长 4.8%，增速较上月收窄 0.7 个百分点，低于
wind 一致预期的 5.32%。分行业看，采矿业、制造业、电力、热力、燃气及
水生产和供应业的工业增加值同比较上月均边际走弱，增速分别回落至 0.7%、
5.4%、4.4%。但制造业工业增加值同比相比近十年季节性水平略高，表明生
产端并未出现明显失速。结构上，10 月高技术产业、装备制造业工业增加值
同比分别增长 17.6%、10.6%。高技术产业增速创下近 5 年来单月最高增速，
其中电子工业专用设备制造、计算机外围设备制造、电子器件制造等行业增加
值分别增长 46.8%、44.7%、34.8%，显示电子信息产业链仍处于较高景气区
间。装备制造业 1-10 月累计增加值占全部规模以上工业比重为 37.9%，同比
提高 1.8 个百分点。细分行业中，10 月单月，计算机、通信和其他电子设备制

造业、专用设备制造业、石油和天然气开采业、铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他
运输设备制造业增加值同比分别增长 19.4%、11.5%、9.4%、9.2%，在主要
行业中增幅居前。金属制品业（8.6%）、食品制造业（8.6%）、医药制造业（5.9%）
增速均边际回升。非金属矿物制品业（-2.3%）、煤炭开采和洗选业（-4.4%）
同比降幅均小幅收窄。这反映出工业生产的改善并非全面扩散，传统周期性行
业仍受到需求不足、价格偏弱等因素制约，产业间景气分化较为明显。从产品
看，“人工智能+”带动产业链上下游产量较快增长，既体现在集成电路
（+21.9%）、电子元件（+15.2%）等核心硬件持续扩张，也体现在工业机器人
（+26.0%）、 3D 打印设备（+85.9%）等高端制造设备加快放量，以及地面通
信导航定向设备（+61.5%）等智能终端产品快速增长，反映出 AI产业发展正
由底层硬件向智能制造和多元化应用场景延伸。出口亦对工业生产形成正向带
动。10 月规模以上工业企业出口交货值同比增长 18.6%，增速较上月加快 1.6

个百分点。装备制造业出口交货值增长 20.6%，增速较上月加快 3.4 个百分点，
其中，汽车、电气机械、电子行业出口交货值分别增长 52.2%、23.7%、15.3%，
对全部规上工业出口交货值增长的贡献率居各大类行业前三位，合计达
66.7%，说明出口对工业生产的拉动并未明显减弱，且支撑力量主要集中在装
备制造和电子产业链。综合来看，年内工业增加值增速或温和放缓，结构升级
趋势明确，高技术制造业和装备制造业是核心支撑。在强出口和稳投资政策发
力下，单月增速有望小幅回升，但难以扭转全年较前三季度小幅放缓的格局。
 整体投资降幅进一步扩大，重点基建领域维持较高景气。2026 年 1-10 月，全
国固定资产投资（不含农户）同比下降 8.1%，降幅较 1-9 月扩大 0.9 个百分
点。根据历史数据倒推测算，10 月单月投资同比下降 17.84%，降幅较上月扩
大 8.16 个百分点。累计降幅扩大相对温和，但单月降幅明显加深，反映近期
投资下行压力仍在加大。基建投资方面，1-10 月广义基建投资同比下降 5.1%，

降幅较 1-9 月扩大 0.9 个百分点；根据历史数据倒推测算，10 月单月广义基建
投资同比下降 17.63%，降幅较上月扩大 7.15 个百分点。资金端来看，10 月
地方政府新增专项债发行 3280 亿元，同比涨幅收窄至 14.13%，发行进度
88.07%，慢于去年同期 2.03 个百分点。专项债发行节奏放缓，叠加前期资金
向项目建设转化存在时滞，基建投资尚未形成回升趋势。尽管广义基建投资整
体负增长，但重点领域投资仍保持较快增长。1-10 月互联网和相关服务业投资
同比增长 48.4%，增速较 1-9 月份加快 1.7 个百分点，反映数字基础设施建设
仍有较强投资需求；航空运输业、水上运输业投资均增长 12.5%，电力供应业
投资增长 9.9%，说明“两重”项目建设及“六张网”相关领域投资仍在推进。
往后看，在四季度财政资金集中拨付、重大项目加速形成实物工作量的支撑下，
11、12 月单月投资增速环比有望小幅改善，带动全年降幅较 1-10 月略有收窄，
但全年转正的概率较低。基建投资中部分结构性高增领域可能受低基数、项目

集中开工影响，持续性需观察。同时，专项债剩余额度在年内可能对基建形成
一定支撑，但不宜高估其扭转深度负增的能力。
 地产供需两端低位运行，投资意愿与在建规模仍偏弱。2026 年 1-10 月，全国
房地产开发投资同比下降 21.6%，降幅较 1-9 月扩大 1.0 个百分点；据历史数
据倒推测算，10月单月房地产开发投资同比下降 34.24%，降幅较上月扩大 7.04
个百分点，地产对整体投资的拖累有所加深，是投资端最大拖累项之一。供给
端，根据历史数据倒推测算，10 月房屋施工面积同比下降 32.5%，降幅较上
月扩大 13.9 个百分点，反映在建规模明显收缩；新开工面积同比下降 18.0%，
降幅收窄 8.1 个百分点，是供给端唯一边际改善项。新开工绝对增速仍深度负
增，且可能受低基数、项目集中开工影响，房企投资意愿仍未实质性修复；竣
工面积同比下降 39.1%，降幅扩大 2.4 个百分点，反映项目交付端压力或边际
上升。需求端，根据历史数据倒推测算，10 月商品房销售面积同比下降 20.7%，

降幅较上月收窄 1.9 个百分点；销售额同比下降 20.5%，降幅收窄 0.6 个百分
点。销售额降幅收窄幅度小于销售面积，意味着销售均价同比表现相对偏弱，
销售端的量价修复仍不充分。同时，销售端边际改善与投资、施工、竣工端恶
化形成背离。说明销售回款尚未有效转化为房企拿地、开工和施工强度，房企
流动性和投资意愿仍是核心约束。资金端，据历史数据倒推测算，10 月单月
房地产开发资金来源同比下降 22.0%，降幅较上月进一步扩大。其中国内贷款、
自筹资金、定金及预收款分别下降 33.0%、22.0%、15.6%，资金链仍在全面

3
收缩。往后看，年内稳地产政策以公积金改革、核心城市限购优化、购房贴息
等需求端工具为主，供给端主要依赖收储去库存，年底地产投资降幅仍在寻顶，
降幅收窄时点或推迟到 2027 年。
 整体企业资本开支意愿偏弱，高技术制造投资稳健增长。2026 年 1-10 月，制
造业投资同比下降 2.9%，降幅较 1-9 月扩大 0.8 个百分点；据历史数据倒推
测算，10 月单月制造业投资同比下降 9.32%，降幅较上月扩大 8.38 个百分点。
结合本月工业生产数据看，当前生产端的增长尚未充分转化为企业新增资本开
支，制造业投资意愿相对谨慎。分行业看，1-10 月高技术制造业投资同比增长
3.0%，其中电子电路制造业投资增长 59.6%，锂离子电池、集成电路制造业
投资分别增长 18.0%、13.8%；高技术服务业投资增长 5.9%，其中与人工智
能等新兴产业密切相关的信息服务业投资增长 20.5%，显示科技产业投资持续
向电子信息制造和人工智能相关服务领域集中。“两新”政策持续落地显效，

设备购置投资规模不断扩大。1-10 月，设备工器具购置投资同比增长 8.9%；
占全部投资的比重为 20.3%，比上年同期提高 3.2 个百分点。10 月单月，金属
制品业、有色金属冶炼及压延加工业投资增速同比分别下降 8.5%和 2.5%，降
幅较上月分别收窄 8.2 和 6.8 个百分点。铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设
备制造业、汽车制造业、通用设备制造业当月投资同比分别下降 0.2%、21.8%、
21.2%，降幅分别扩大 12.7、13.6、23.5 个百分点。装备制造业内部投资分化
加剧，终端需求偏弱、价格竞争加剧、产能利用率不足，以及前期抢出口和“以
旧换新”带来的需求前置，可能共同影响企业扩产意愿，促使企业资本开支趋
于谨慎。对比来看，10 月装备制造业工业增加值仍增长 10.6%，反映出当前
装备制造业呈现生产保持较强韧性、投资扩张意愿不足的特征，生产端与投资
端的背离进一步加深。往后看，高技术制造业投资动能有望延续，四季度制造
业投资单月同比有望转正，但全年增速明显转正难度较大；设备购置、电子电

路、锂电、集成电路和 AI 相关信息服务是主要支撑，汽车、通用设备和传统
制造或成为主要拖累。
 消费总量增长继续放缓，部分品类受到价格和低基数扰动。10 月单月，社会
消费品零售总额同比增长 0.4%，增速较上月收窄 0.7 个百分点；扣除汽车后
的消费品零售额同比增长 2.5%，增速较上月收窄 1.4 个百分点。分项看，商
品零售同比增长 0.3%，增速较上月收窄 0.7 个百分点；餐饮收入同比增长
0.7%，增速收窄 1.4 个百分点，整体消费需求仍偏弱。分品类看，汽车类零售
额同比下降 21.4%，降幅较上月扩大 0.2 个百分点，成为社零增长的主要拖累，
反映居民大额耐用品消费意愿不足。通讯器材类零售同比增长 6.5%，增速较
上月大幅回落 24.6 个百分点，说明前期高增长后基数效应明显，补贴效应和
换机需求均有所减弱。金银珠宝类零售同比下降 28.7%，降幅较上月扩大 19.1
个百分点，主要因近期金价回调压低零售额读数，价格因素大于需求因素，也
反映出金银珠宝消费对金价波动高度敏感。石油及制品类零售同比由下降转为

上升 4.4%，主要受近期油价上涨叠加基数因素影响。家用电器和音像器材类
零售同比上升 2.5%，家具类零售同比下降 2.6%，增速均边际回升，或受去年
“以旧换新”退潮带来的低基数影响。尽管消费总量增速有所回落，消费结构
升级和服务消费韧性仍在延续。1-10 月，社会消费品和服务零售总额同比增长
2.5%，其中服务零售额增长 4.9%，高于商品零售额 4.0 个百分点。往后看，
以旧换新政策边际效应减弱、居民消费意愿回升偏慢等因素或持续拖累社零整
体增速。年内消费韧性更多取决于服务消费能否延续较快增速，以及即时零售、
银发经济等新增长点能否对冲传统商品消费偏弱的拖累，托举社零中枢维持在
温和区间。
 风险提示：政策落地节奏不及预期风险，海外经济超预期波动风险。已公布的
宏观或产业政策在实际执行过程中，可能因配套措施不完善、部门协调不畅、
地方执行力度差异或市场环境变化等因素，导致其推进速度、覆盖范围或最终
效果慢于或弱于市场预期，从而影响政策目标的实现与经济修复的进程；主要
经济体的政治局势、经济数据或宏观政策出现意外变化，其幅度或频率超出市

场普遍预测。这种超预期波动可能引发全球金融市场动荡、贸易条件恶化或外
需急剧收缩，并通过金融、贸易及预期渠道向国内传导，增加我国外部环境的
复杂性与不稳定性。

分析师承诺 
本报告署名分析师具有中国证券业协会授予的证券投资咨询执业资格并注册为证券分析师，报告所采用的数据均
来自合法合规渠道，分析逻辑基于分析师的职业理解，通过合理判断得出结论，独立、客观地出具本报告。分析师承
诺不曾因，不因，也将不会因本报告中的具体推荐意见或观点而直接或间接获取任何形式的补偿。

投资评级说明 
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为公司评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内
的相对市场表现，即：以报告发布日后 6 个月内公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅
作为基准。其中：A 股市场以沪深 300 指数为基准，新三板市场以三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针
对做市转让标的）为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准；美国市场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数为基准。 
公司评级 
买入：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 20%以上 
持有：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 10%与 20%之间 
中性：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于-10%与 10%之间 
回避：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于-20%与-10%之间 
卖出：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在-20%以下 
行业评级 
强于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报高于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 5%以上 
跟随大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报介于同期相关证券市场代表性指数-5%与 5%之间 
弱于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报低于同期相关证券市场代表性指数-5%以下

### Page 3

3
Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 政治風險, 聽證會與法案: 黃仁勳赴國會及參議院聽證, 暗示短期難以回歸; 年底NDAA法案包裹一系列
晶片相關限制等小法案, 恐進一步壓抑海外需求並加速供應鏈分化。
• 輝達量化緊縮, 連鎖效應擴散: 輝達明年營收增長率至75%, 且採購承諾將如期進行。不過新興雲端與
規模較小公司恐面臨較嚴格融資擔保條件。
• Fed助美元, 亞幣呈分化: 國際美元強勢, 但亞幣走出自己的路, 出口暢旺卻強弱分歧, 台日雙疲, 中韓強
勢, 估計該現象將延續至年底。
2. 後續看點:
• APEC會談結果(11/20)、美國PCE(11/25), OPEC+會議(11/29)。
策略思維
1) 股市−增速放緩, 但成長格局未變: 市場對AI投資效益及資料中心建置疑慮升溫, 科技股大幅修正。然而, 目
前主要瓶頸來自供給, 而非需求轉弱; 預計2028年EPS減速而不致負成長, 震盪放大但長多行情不致改變。
2) 債市−高利中樞環境成為常態: 升息預期、財政紀律及資金環境趨緊, 均將影響利率環境higher for longer; 惟

在市場波動加劇、高利優勢背景下, 固定收益資產配置價值仍高, 報酬以現金流為主。
3) 匯市−短線美元偏強震盪: 升息預期帶動美元指數走強, Fed官員偏鷹基調亦將對美元形成支撐。若未來地緣
溢價風險能延續降溫、油價走勢緩步回落, 將限縮美元指數大幅上行空間。

Cathay’s View
AI投資驅動半導體市場規模擴大 市場擔憂2028年投資放緩, 半導體銷售動能也將下滑
註: 8大CSP包括谷歌、微軟、亞馬遜、Meta、甲骨文、SpaceX、CoreWeave、Nebius。
2026年全球半導體銷售為今年前9月數據年化後結果。
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/11/17。
• 民主黨勝選後, 市場開始擔憂AI監管政策可能影響資料中心興建進度, 如今輝達代理執行長又表示將更加審慎檢視
未來AI供應鏈投資方案, 進一步加深市場對AI資本支出放緩的疑慮。從半導體市場規模來看, 近年AI投資熱潮已帶
動產業快速擴張, 全球半導體銷售額從2025年的約7,250億美元, 預估攀升至2026年超過1.3兆美元, 顯示AI已成為
推動半導體產業成長的重要引擎。
• 然而, 市場真正擔憂的並非今年半導體需求是否持續成長, 而是2028年之後的成長動能能否延續。隨著大型CSP業
者逐步檢視資本支出效益, 加上資料中心興建可能受到融資成本與監管政策影響, 未來AI投資增速存在放緩風險,

進而使半導體銷售成長面臨壓力。
AI循環｜ AI投資驅動半導體版圖擴大, 惟市場擔心增速放緩
註: 8大CSP包括谷歌、微軟、亞馬遜、Meta、甲骨文、SpaceX、CoreWeave、Nebius。

• AI產業短線面臨諸多事件考驗：
• 產業焦點將轉向政策風險。黃仁勳赴國會及參議院聽證, 顯示AI監管與國安議題升溫; 年底NDAA法案包裹一系列
晶片相關限制小法案, 恐進一步壓抑海外需求並加速供應鏈分化。2027年新一屆國會上任後, 相關監管與管制措施

### Page 7

7
Cathay’s View
輝達的財務承諾及融資平台 市場擔心AI基建放緩, 股價評價下修
資料來源：Bloomberg,  截至2026/11/16。
• 輝達更換執行長後, 市場最關注的焦點之一, 就是先前承諾的AI相關融資計畫是否能夠持續推進, 包括已承諾的合
約, 以及規模高達5,000億美元的融資平台能否如期落實。一旦新任經營團隊對資本配置採取更審慎的態度, 相關
融資與投資承諾可能逐步放緩, 直接的影響就是資料中心的擴建速度將同步放緩。輝達最新財報中顯示, 財務承諾
金額從上季的3660億美元, 提高至5180億美元, 仍將如期進行。
• 對金融市場而言, 資料中心建置放緩, 也將導致市場下修AI需求的成長潛力, 估值也面臨同步下修的壓力, 後續將持
續關注新任執行長對投資策略及新國會後的政府動向。不過, 值得注意的是, 輝達放緩AI投資, 反而有助於輝達債
信品質的提升。
AI融資｜ 輝達目前仍維持既有融資策略, 但關注未來新任執行長的意向
資料來源：輝達財報Q3財報發布會, 截至2026/11/17。

### Page 8

8
Cathay’s View
• 新任執行長表示, 將對新興雲(Neocloud)給予「有紀律、具選擇性」的支持, 業者須先自行取得融資、完成算力部
署, 才能取得輝達320億美元的擔保承諾。預估2027年新興雲資本支出900-1000億美元, 其中CoreWeave和
Nebius預估明年的資本支出將達850億美元, 並已表示將透過客戶訂金、長約及GPU向銀行抵押融資, 取得7-8成
以上資金。其他新興雲規模較小, 即使無法取得輝達擔保承諾, 對明年整體高達1.5兆美元的AI資本支出影響有限。
• 輝達放緩AI投資, 對輝達營收和股票評價面是利空, 但對債信反而是正面影響, 輝達CDS在黃仁勳辭職後出現回落,
預期資金將流向大型CSP, 加速形成算力寡占市場。新興雲融資仰賴客戶長約與GPU抵押價值, 因此GPU租金維持
上漲、算力需求保持強勁成長, 將是未來能否降低AI融資風險的因素。
輝達新興雲擔保承諾及新興雲預估資本支出
資料來源: 輝達, Bloomberg預估, 預估範圍為2027年, 截至2026/11/18。

台股評價面下調壓力沉重 資金flight-to-quality, 台積電亦遭大舉賣壓
註: 預估本益比=股價/未來一年預估EPS。

• Semi analysis大幅下修2028-2030年AI晶片資本支出3-4成, 同時CSP資本支出年增率預估在2028-2030年僅個位

• 此外, 市場恐慌情緒攀升, 資金轉向現金避險, 台積電亦遭拋售, 股市賣壓沉重但缺少低接買盤, 提高融資斷頭及多
殺多風險, 台股融資餘額從高點6400億元銳減至5080億元。

悲觀情境: 台積電2028年預估獲利從34%減至個位數 台積電啟動長期擴廠計畫, 反映對AI需求長期看好
註: 2027年預估EPS持平, 2028年下修15%。
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 預估範圍為2027-2028年, 截至2026/11/18。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 預估範圍為2027-2028年, 截至2026/11/18。
• 市場對CSP資本支出增速放緩已形成初步共識, 並開始反映2028年半導體獲利下修風險。預估台積電2027年獲利
年增34%, 2028年受CSP投資放緩影響, EPS年增率將由26%降至個位數, 全年EPS預估160元。以11/19收盤價
2100元計算, 前瞻本益比僅約13倍, 處於歷史低檔, 股價拉回宜以買方思維看待。然而, 其他台股電子族群仍面臨
輝達供應鏈重組、零組件供需及漲價預期修正等壓力, 估值落底仍需時間, 短期震盪及反彈壓力較大。
• 台積電將赴美國德州擴廠, 並重申AI需求具備多年強勁成長動能, 反映對2030年後AI長期需求仍具信心。此外,

CSP於2028年適度放緩投資, 有助於延長AI產業成長週期, 並不代表半導體需求趨勢反轉。

增加, 記憶體股上漲動能放緩, 不過AI長期發展仍帶動獲利成長, 股價拉回接近4月起漲點可站買方思考。

Cathay’s View
近期聯準會官員談話 市場升息預期上修, 已積極預估至明年升逾3碼
註: 摘錄今年投票委員談話。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/11/18。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/17。
• 聯準會維持鷹派立場: 除理事Waller考慮12月升息50bps、聖路易斯Fed總裁Musalem支持2027年春季前多次升息
之外, 多數官員仍支持循序漸進的緊縮, 料12月升息1碼仍為基準情境。主席Warsh則探討AI發展的可能性, 儘管AI
效益轉化仍有待驗證, 但長期正向成長趨勢明確, 並將在未來一段期間內持續推升通膨壓力。
• 市場已提前反映未來升息空間: 現行市場預期Fed至2027年Q3累計升息3~4碼, 反映通膨黏著及經濟韌性。惟若能
源價格續跌或供需狀況緩步改善, 終端利率預估值或有調整空間。
• 高利環境持續成為常態: 通膨方面, 撇除地緣衝突影響外, 2027年AI投資動能仍將支撐經濟成長; 而美國財政赤字

及AI基礎建設再融資均推升公債/信用債供給量, 預期長天期利率續居高位。
美國利率｜官員談話延續鷹派立場, 升息預期持續支撐美債利率高檔
官員 公開談話
Michelle Bowman
(副主席, 11/10)
經濟、就業市場仍具韌性, 現階段通膨風險仍高, 而
預計2027年通膨壓力將減弱。
Christopher Waller
(理事, 11/10)
若核心CPI月增持續維持在0.3%以上, 12月應考慮一
次升息50bps, 目前限制性不足。
Kevin Warsh
(主席, 11/16)
AI發展情境推估:
1)推升經濟成長、生產力, 企業與勞工共同受益。
2)經濟活動擴張, 但正面效益與大規模失業抵銷。
3)AI發展未達預期, 引發股市下跌及經濟放緩。
Beth Hammack
(克里夫蘭, 11/16) 傾向於緩慢、穩步推進的方式小幅升息。
John Williams
(紐約, 11/18) 經濟勢頭增強、通膨居高不下, 但無需大幅升息。

ECB接班人傾向 2027/2歐美升息預期對比
資料來源: Econotream, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/11/17。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/10。
歐美匯率｜ECB接班對比Fed偏緊態度
• 拉加德人事變動短期雖不致改變ECB的年底升息路徑, 但因呼聲較高的接任者, 目前是偏鴿的前西班牙總裁, 加以
歐元區內部(法國預算)的金融穩定正成為限制ECB明年2月後持續升息的煞車。此外立場向來強硬的拉脫維亞總裁
卡扎克斯日前都開始強調「成長代價」與「微妙權衡」, 這向市場傳遞了一個重要訊號, 12月升息後, 利率將觸及
或略高於中性利率區間(1.75%～2.50%)上限, 2027年初若要再繼續升息, 門檻將會明顯提高。
• 對比美元受惠於美國第三季GDP成長3.4%的強勁韌性及黏著通膨, 支撐聯準會維持鷹派; 反觀歐元區第三季 GDP
僅增 0.4%, 加上法國債市風險, 歐美貨幣政策在2027年初的預期落差, 正壓抑短期歐元走勢, 支持美元高位偏強。

美元兌四國匯率走勢 四國出口年增率
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/17。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10。
• 中、韓、日、台四國近期受惠於全球貿易與AI科技需求, 出口表現普遍暢旺, 但匯率走勢卻呈現分化。
• 台日雙疲: 資金外流與政策掣肘而陷入「出口旺、匯率弱」的背離困境。新台幣受到外資在台股大舉獲利了結並
淨匯出百億美元, 即便台灣央行頻頻進場調節仍難敵龐大賣壓; 日圓則在160關卡前低檔徘徊, 由於日本第三季
GDP意外萎縮削弱升息底氣, 加上市場對日本財務省干預漸消, 美日利差套利交易再度壓抑日圓表現。
• 中韓雙強: 韓元稱霸主要受惠出口改善, 於韓國企業響應公司治理改革, 大舉實施庫藏股並將海外盈餘與貨款匯回
國內, 形成強勁的實質換匯需求; 人民幣則在出口高成長帶動下, 受惠於中國人民銀行順應市場供需「放手」調升
中間價, 而這些因素並非單一事件而是持續發生, 後續四個貨幣的走法也隨其持續機會較大。

• 輝達整體財報優於預期：2026年Q3 (2026/7/26-2026/10/25)營收再創新高, 從前期的962億美元提升至1158億美
元(預期1090億美元), GAAP EPS從2.46美元提高至2.70美元, 毛利率為74.8%, 均優於預期。展望2026年
Q4(2026/10/25-2027/1/31), 預期營收將達1300億美元, 不過受到記憶體價格上漲影響, 毛利率受到擠壓而下滑至
71.5%, 但後續有望改善。
• 從產品別來看, 資料中心仍是營收成長的主要驅動力, 從前期的890億美元上升至1076億美元, 年增率達110%, 營
收佔比維持在9成。另外, 輝達上修明年的營收成長預估, 從70%提高至75%。

美國零售銷售和控制組月增率 消費類股強弱與標普指數
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10, 虛線為滾動3ma。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/19。
美國消費股｜市場吹起避險風, 資金流向核心消費
• 近期核心零售展現韌性的背景下, 必需消費類股在當前市場中展現出優勢。全美汽油均價略下滑, 省下的可支配現
金重新導向日常剛性採買。帶動10月核心零售銷售月增0.6%、年增5.6%, 表現優於整體零售數據, 這些日用品等
領域為必需消費產業奠定基礎。
• 另一方面, 當前市場正處於風險趨避氛圍, 高估值AI科技成長股因資本支出檢視而震盪加劇, 在「基本面直接受惠
油價下跌」與「低Beta避險屬性」的雙重加持下, 消費類股成為當前進可攻、退可守的優勢配置。

中國｜阿里財報不如預期, 黃仁勳下台衝擊「外訓內推」模式, 拖累大盤
• 阿里巴巴財報揭曉, 營收弱於預期, 印證電商「內需逆風」的窘境: 1)傳統核心電商: 「淘天集團」受制於內需疲弱,
呼應社融與消費數據陰霾, 平台營收成長力道不強; 2) AI相關的「阿里雲」: 具抗週期韌性, 因為企業為了生存反而
迫切導入AI來「降本增效」, 加上雲端系統具備高轉換成本與訂閱制鎖定慣性, 成為財報中少數亮點。
• 美國禁售高階AI晶片, 使阿里採取「外訓內推」的灰色突圍模式。 也就是在東南亞資料中心租用高階算力完成大
模型預訓練, 再將模型用國產晶片進行日常推理服務, 藉此在技術上緊咬國際巨頭。但隨黃仁勳下台、美國恐關閉

第三地算力通道, 恐讓大模型研發進度延遲, 股價單日重挫近7%。展望後市, 在財報不如預期與算力受阻的雙重夾
擊下, 阿里股價目前仍是弱勢格局。此外, 大規模財政刺激暫時無望, 長期均線空頭排列下, A股大盤目前也尚未逆
轉弱勢格局。
資料來源: Wind, 截至2026/11/17。 資料來源: Wind, 截至2026/11/17。

USD HY發行(左軸)
市場對信用審視趨於嚴謹, CCC利差持續走擴 AI融資需求仍推升信用債供給
註: 信用利差BB=182bps、B=311bps、CCC=1083bps。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/16。
註: 1)採總發行(Gross Issuance); 截至2026/11/5, hyperscalers已發行2960億美元。
2)預估IG/HY總發行: 2026年2.3兆美元/3400億美元; 2027年2.4兆美元/3750億美元。
資料來源: 高盛預估, 截至2026/11。
• 隨利率中樞上移及融資成本攀升, 低評級企業再融資壓力明顯增加, CCC利差持續高於1000bps, YTD擴張幅度明

顯高於BB及B級。然而, 目前整體HY利差約320bps,仍明顯低於歷次衰退前夕常見的500~700bps區間。反映市場
對利率水準升高的重新定價, 而非出現對硬著陸的系統性擔憂。
• 未來供給壓力仍存, 所幸增幅有望放緩: Hyperscalers發債規模持續增加, 預估2027年發行量將升破4000億美元,
持續形成信用供給壓力; 然而, 隨資料中心建設成長高峰逐步趨緩, 2028年AI資本支出增速有望降溫, 亦有助緩和市
場對現金流的疑慮。在市場已提前反映供給壓力下, 且未來有望增速趨緩前提下, 預期信用利差將維持高檔震盪,
而非持續大幅走擴。配置上, 建議優先布局基本面穩健之IG以及HY中的BB發行人, 並審慎看待CCC券種。
信用債｜CCC信用利差大幅走擴; 未來供給壓力仍存, 惟增幅有望逐步趨緩

市場焦點
美國經濟
• 第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。美國9月個人消
費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，年增3.6%；核心PCE指數月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對
於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，
持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，
將提高川普後續推動政策的難度。觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年
通常是股市表現最佳的年度，其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對

政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
中國
• 9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快，中國工業利潤改善，主要由AI
硬體與能源價格上漲帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企業則是能
源價格上漲的最大受益者之一，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線，但內需疲
弱與價格競爭問題未解，投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。

美國第三季GDP成長超預期、通膨壓力續增
• 美國第三季經濟成長率(GDP)創一年來最大增幅：美國經濟分析局(BEA)公布，第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場
估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。企業AI投資火熱、消費者開支保持韌性，抵銷了油價上漲衝擊。
• 9月數據顯示通膨壓力續增，但符合預期：美國9月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，寫3月以來最大月增幅；年增幅
3.6%。剔除食物與能源的9月核心PCE指數，月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 市場反應：由於新一輪通膨數據符合預期，市場未改變聯準會12月利率決議升息一碼的預測。然而，有跡象顯示實質個人
消費支出開始轉弱，儲蓄率也有所下降，市場開始押注通膨可能已經見頂。從聯邦利率期貨市場數據來看，市場對於明年升

息空間的預期已較上個月收斂(九月底預測升息2~3碼→目前預測1~2碼)，
• 觀點：美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，
可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
資料來源：Bloomberg，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.30。

聯準會貨幣決議與聲明稿差異
資料來源：Federal Reserve（10/28、9/16 聲明）；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。以上為聲明重點整理。
2 / 5
10月決議｜按兵不動
3.75% – 4.00%
10票贊成、2票反對
經濟措辭轉強 「穩健擴張」改為「強勁擴張」；國內支出具韌性， 生產力與資本投資表現強。
通膨仍未解除 聲明維持「通膨居高」的判斷，未再宣稱本次升息有 助加快回到2%目標。
政策訊號更具彈性 改稱需要更多資訊判斷適當政策立場，未承諾下一步 升息或降息。
關鍵變化
理事庫克(Lisa D. Cook) 和克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth M. Hammack)主張本次升息1碼，
形成2張鷹派反對票；9月決議則為一致通過。利率不變不等於轉向寬鬆，委員會內部對通膨風險仍

有不同判斷。
市場解讀：本次是「暫停觀察」；12月是否再升息，仍取決於通膨、需求與能源價格。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
資料來源：Federal Reserve 10/28會後記者會；Reuters、Investing.com，2026/10/28。
3 / 5
01｜市場利率反映緊縮壓力
華許指出，9月與10月兩次會議之
間，美國公債名目與實質殖利率明
顯走高；市場利率可先行反映成長
、通膨與風險變化，不必等政策利
率調整。
政策含意
長天期殖利率仍受通膨預期、期限
溢酬及公債供需影響；政策利率不
變，不代表債市壓力解除。
02｜AI投資與生產力
華許認為美國經濟尚未過熱，AI投
資推升需求，但生產力也有改善；
聯準會將持續評估新科技對產出、
就業與物價的中長期影響。
政策含意
AI支出可支撐企業成長，也伴隨資本
支出回收與融資風險；應檢視現金流
、投資報酬及生產力是否實際提升。

03｜政策制度與工具再檢視
聯準會規劃2027年安排1次、2028
年安排2次專注貨幣政策策略的會
議，不進行利率表決或發布政策聲
明；另將檢視資產負債表政策。
政策含意
制度調整不等於政策轉向寬鬆；仍須
觀察準備金、資產負債表操作及經濟
數據，評估金融條件如何變化。
解讀：華許除談利率外，也聚焦市場利率、AI生產力及政策制度調整；投資判斷仍應回到數據、企業獲利與金融條件。

• 美國期中選舉登場，國會席次變化牽動川普施政：美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，為川普第二任期上任以來首場全國性選
舉，除國會議員改選外，亦包括州長及其他公職人員選舉。若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，將提
高川普後續推動政策的難度。不過，川普仍可透過發布行政命令等方式，在一定程度上繞過國會的立法制衡，持續推動其政

策議程。
• 本次選舉結果可能耗費一週才能明朗：回顧2006至2022年近五屆美國期中選舉，其中四屆的參眾兩院控制權於投票當晚或
隔日即大致明朗，僅2022年因關鍵席次競爭激烈、郵寄選票計票耗時較長，直至投票後第8天才確認兩院席次歸屬。考量本
次參議院選情膠著，加上川普執政以來美國政治極化持續加劇，不排除本次選舉結果需耗費一週才能明朗，期間市場也可能
面臨額外的不確定性。

期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度
• 期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度：觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳
的年度。根據1940至2025年統計，標普500指數第三年平均報酬率達16.5%，高於第一年的8.3%、第二年的4.2%及第四年
的6.9%；中位數報酬率更達18.9%。其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，
且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
• 政黨與國會組成影響有限，基本面仍是關鍵： 進一步觀察政黨差異，1940年以來，第三年由共和黨總統執政時，標普500指
數平均報酬率約18.2%，高於民主黨總統執政時的14.9%，但兩者均呈現正報酬，顯示總統所屬政黨並非決定股市表現的唯

一因素。國會組成亦可能影響政策推動與市場預期，例如共和黨總統搭配民主黨國會時，第三年平均報酬率約15.6%；若國
會呈現混合執政，平均報酬率則達23.1%。

能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升
• 能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升：9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快。但前三季度累計增速
從1到8月的15.7%小幅放緩至15.5%，利潤改善主要反映了能源和AI硬體價格的上漲，其中AI硬體價格仍是高技術製造業利潤
的重要支撐。前三季度，高技術製造業利潤年成長52.0%，與1到8月54.7%的增速大體相當。拉動整體利潤成長約10個百分點，
貢獻率約三分之二。值得注意的是，利潤成長仍主要集中於少數相關行業，其他企業部門整體依然處境艱難，內需疲弱疊加價
格競爭激烈令利潤受到擠壓。
• 投資建議：中國工業利潤改善，主要由AI硬體與能源價格上漲帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企

業則是能源價格上漲的最大受益者之一，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線，但內需疲弱與價格競爭問題未解，
投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.27
能源、AI價格飆升提振中國相關行業利潤增速

中國科技公司享受負殖利率差
• 中國科技公司享受負殖利率差：中國在經濟成長放緩與寬鬆貨幣
政策下，借貸成本持續下降，10年期公債殖利率由年初1.86%降
至1.63%，使企業融資條件更加寬鬆，相較之下，美國受經濟韌
性與高利率政策影響，10年期公債殖利率升至約5%，企業整體融
資成本明顯提高。此外2025年中國大型科技企業在境內債券市場
融資規模有限，僅為約74億美元，而且支付的殖利率，平均比同
風 險 層 級 的 債 券 高 出18個 基 點，但現在溢 價 已 經 變 為 折 價 ，
2026年迄今，中國科技企業已在境內發債約100億美元，新發科
技債殖利率平均折價6個基點，顯示機構投資者信心發生了顯著轉
變，大幅降低了行業推進AI建設的資金成本，會有上述現象是由
於中國經濟通縮，難以承受更高利率，因此官方須維持較寬鬆的

金融條件，科技企業剛剛開始通過債券市場融資，預料債券將受
到投資者關注。
• 展望後市：低成本債券融資對中國科技行業構成利好，與IPO不
同，債券市場為企業提供了一個可重複利用的融資渠道，在股市
劇烈波動之際，對投資人是另一種資產配置，可優先關注具資金
優勢與應用場景的大型科技龍頭，以及受惠於AI算力需求成長的
伺服器、網通設備和資料中心產業。

中美國債走勢分化

AI產業鏈融資警訊：資金缺口正在擴散
• 資本支出與現金流的嚴重失衡：AI生態系中的巨額投資正考驗著企業，一旦企業營運現金流不足以支持基礎設施建設，就必
須高度依賴外部融資(發債或增資)，使其更容易受到資本市場波動與利率變化的衝擊。AI算力提供商資金缺口最為顯著，其
資本支出已接近營運現金流3倍(284%)，需要投入大額資金採購高端GPU並對外出租算力，數據中心資本支出佔現金流的比
例已從2022年11月(ChatGPT問世)1倍(104%)快速爬升至1.3倍(128%)，超大規模雲服務商資本支出占現金流比例已從2022
年底40%翻倍飆升至73%。在彭博研究統計的8家超大型雲業者中，出現自由現金流為負(資金缺口)公司數量已從2022年的
1家增加至4家(達50%)。
• 產業鏈錯綜複雜，風險具連鎖效應：從上游晶片製造商、硬體開發商、建築商、電力/公用事業，到中游的數據中心與雲端

服務商，再到下游的軟體與數據管理公司，其銷售與支付合約彼此高度緊密相連。當算力提供商或數據中心出現資金吃緊時，
風險會迅速向上游傳遞，導致項目延期、融資成本攀升，甚至延後整個生態系的收入實現時程。目前除了雲端與數據中心外，
硬體開發商與建築商中出現資金缺口的企業比例也在上升。
資金缺口正蔓延至人工智能產業鏈一半的層級
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
算力提供商和數據中心的資本支出超過現金流

市場定價轉變：借貸成本上升與股票估值修正
• 資本市場已經開始針對資金缺口進行風險定價：在資金缺口最大的AI算力提供商、數據中心以及超大規模雲服務商中，其發
行的公司債相較於美國國債的殖利率差(利差)顯著拓寬，高於一般投資級債券或高收益債券的表現，顯示債券市場對其違約
與融資風險要求更高的風險補償。由於大量現金流被基礎設施建設掏空，且AI應用端的獲利回收速度未達市場預期，投資人
不再願意給予極高溢價，整體AI產業鏈的預期本益比出現明顯修正。
• 小結與投資建議：當前AI產業正在經歷基礎設施過渡期，資本密集的算力出租、數據中心建設正遭受強烈的資金擠壓，若未
來1~2年內下游應用端的商業化營收無法順利銜接，依賴高度槓桿與外部融資的企業將面臨嚴峻的流動性挑戰。對AI算力出
租商及部分高負債的數據中心開發商保持謹慎態度，一旦信貸市場收緊或借貸成本升高，利潤與營運將受重創。晶片製造商

的經營現金流持續成長，財務結構改善，選擇擁有強大訂價權與現金流護城河的上游半導體巨頭，安全邊際最高，電力與公
用事業資本支出佔現金流比例相對穩定且受惠於剛性需求。超大規模雲服務商中表現差異大，優先選擇資產負債表健全、資
產回報率高且擁有傳統核心業務能持續提供穩定現金流的科技巨頭，遠離需大量靠發債維持資本支出的個股。
超大規模雲服務商和數據中心的利差擴大
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
隨著借貸成本波動加劇，AI股權估值走弱

顧 • 利率可能維持高檔更久，但收益價值正在浮現：近月以
來美國10年期公債殖利率一度升抵5.36%，30年期接
近5.7%，反映市場重新評價未來利率中樞、財政赤字
及資金需求等因素。雖然長天期債券價格仍可能受到利
率波動影響，但高殖利率已大幅提高新進場資金的收益
起點，投資焦點應由預測利率轉向評估收益價值。
• 債券標售與資金流顯示配置需求逐步回溫：10月美國
財政部10年期、20年期及30年期公債標售需求維持穩
健，投標倍數約介於2.5至2.8倍區間；同時，美國債券
基金已連續26週呈現資金淨流入，顯示市場未必認為
利率已見頂，但已開始接受5%以上殖利率的長期配置
價值。
• 收益優先取代押注利率轉折：在Higher for Longer環
境下，債券的核心功能重新回到穩定收益、分散風險與

降低組合波動。建議可以3至7年期美國公債及高評等
投資級公司債作為核心收益部位，長天期債券則採分批
布局方式，兼顧收益來源及未來利率循環變化帶來的潛
在機會。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。

非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。

2026 年 10 月 30 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）予測

～ほぼゼロ成長も、緩やかな景気回復は継続～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田 隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表する 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.0%（前期比年
率換算＋0.1%）と予測する。設備投資や政府消費の増加に支えられ、かろうじて 4 四半期連続のプラス成長を
維持したものの、個人消費や輸出が前期の一時的な押し上げ要因の剥落もあって減少したことで、ほぼゼロ成長
にとどまったと見込まれる。なお、輸入が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は大きめのマイナスと
なるものの、公的在庫のプラス寄与により、おおむね相殺されたと考えられる。 
また、名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.9%（年率換算＋3.8%）となったと見込まれる。コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進
む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比＋2.7%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と高めの伸びが続いた。 
今回、7～9 月期の実質 GDP はほぼゼロ成長にとどまる見込みだが、イラン情勢の悪化という逆風を踏まえれ
ば、国内景気は底堅さを維持している。先行きについては、輸入コストの高止まりに伴う価格転嫁の進展が景気
の重しとなる可能性はあるものの、良好な雇用・所得環境や企業業績の改善を背景に内需の回復基調が維持さ
れることなどから、国内景気は緩やかな回復を続けると期待される。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度 
（出所）内閣府「四半期別 GDP 速報」により作成

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－0.1%） 
 良好な所得環境が維持される中、消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かったものの、前期に耐久財
が自動車やエアコンを中心に一時的な要因によって増加した反動が生じたとみられる。価格転
嫁の進展による物価上昇、地震や台風といった自然災害の発生も重しとなった可能性がある。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－0.3%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数が横ばいとなる中、建築コストが上昇していることもあり、住
宅投資は小幅ながら減少が続いたとみられる。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比＋0.9%） 
 企業の投資意欲は省力化やデジタル需要などに支えられて堅調であり、設備投資は 3 四半期
ぶりに増加したと見込まれる。機械設備投資の供給側統計である資本財総供給（除く輸送機械）
も 7～9 月期は前期比＋5.3%と増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度－0.0%ポイント） 
 前期に供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫などの積み増しを進めた反動もあ
り、7～9 月期はマイナス寄与になったと考えられる。 
 なお、公的在庫については、政府による代替調達の進展により石油備蓄の減少ペースが大きく鈍
化したことから、前期比寄与度＋0.5%ポイントとプラス寄与になったとみられる。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費支出（前期比＋0.3%） 
 高齢化が進む中、医療費・介護費を中心に底堅く推移したと予測する。 
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－2.7%） 
 建築・土木コストの上昇が続いている中、7～8 月の建設総合統計（出来高ベース：公共）は弱
めの動きとなったことから、実質公共投資は 2 四半期連続で減少したと見込まれる。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度－0.5%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比－0.5%、実質輸入：同＋1.7%） 
 輸出は前期比－0.5%と減少したと予測する。財は堅調な米国向けや中東向けの持ち直しを受
けて増加した一方、サービスは前期に研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れた反動
で減少したとみられる。 
 輸入は前期比＋1.7%と増加したと予測する。特に財が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直した。ただ
し、サービスについては減少した可能性がある。 
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は－0.5%ポイントとマイナスになったと見込ま
れる。

## Document 120: 261112_gs_inventec

### Page 2

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Neutral rating on Inventec with a new 12m TP of NT$56 (from NT$61), 
based on 14.2x 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E for the past 
10 years, and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or

Lotes released its 3Q26 results on Nov 12th and held a results 
call on Nov 13th. Key takeaways from management include: 
(1) Lotes expects GM to begin recovering starting from 4Q26 along 
with the ramp up of AMD Venice CPU, with 4Q26 GM expected to 
reach similar levels as 2Q26 (GM was 44.8% in 3Q26, down from 
45.5% in 2Q26), and expects 4Q26 revenue to see low single digit 
QoQ growth. 
(2) Management is positive on the outlook for its server business in 
2027, expecting YoY growth to accelerate compared with 2026 
(server business with 57% YoY in 2026E per GSe), and expects 
server business to account for 60%+ of total 2027 revenue (vs. 54% 
in 2026E per GSe). The solid growth will be driven by (1) server unit 
growth of 10-15% in 2027 (up from ~10% in 2026), (2) ASP increase 
across new server CPU products (including server CPU sockets, PCIe 
cables and connectors), and (3) rising contribution from new 
AI-related products (including QD, SOCAMM and new NPO 
solutions). 
(3) The company is conservative on its 2027 GM outlook, indicating 
that overall GM is unlikely to reach the 50%+ level, mainly due to a 
higher contribution from margin-dilutive products (QD and 
SOCAMM) and delay in the ramp up of next gen server CPU products 
(MP timing for Intel Oak Stream has been delayed and will begin by 
late 1Q27 or early 2Q27). 
(4) Despite QD being margin dilutive (QD with 35-40% GM per GSe), 
management is positive that this product will drive revenue/EPS 
growth going forward. Management indicated that QD contributed 
~2% of 3Q26 revenue (with 150k+ pieces of monthly shipment per 
GSe vs. ~15k pieces of shipment in 2025). Management expects 
revenue contribution from QD will increase to 3-5% by December 
and further expand to ~10% in 2027. UTR for QD is currently full, 
and the company plans to double the capacity over the coming

### Page 3

quarters.
(5) The company expects NPO products to enter MP in 2027, although their revenue
contribution is likely to be <2%. Management anticipates revenue contribution to
increase by multiple folds in 2028, driven by broader adoption of 6.4T NPO products,
which are expected to have higher ASP than the mainstream 3.2T NPO products in 2027.
(6) For the CPU platform, Lotes mentioned Eagle Stream’s penetration is currently at
~50% (was 40-50% in 2Q26), while Birch Stream’s penetration is trending upward to

~50% (was ~40% in 2Q26). For AMD, SP5 remains mainstream while current penetration
for Venice is at <3%, and is expected to reach 3-5% by 2026 end. The company now
expects AMD Venice penetration to average 20% in 2027 (previously expect to reach
30-40% by 2027 end) while Intel’s Oak Stream is expected to reach an average
penetration of 10-15% in 2H27 (previously expect to reach 20-30% by 2027 end).
Investment view
We remain relatively cautious about Lotes’ near term business outlook, and we expect

limited room for meaningful margin improvement in the near term, mainly due to (1)
CPU socket shipments remain heavily focused toward legacy products, as the ramp up of
next gen CPU platforms (including AMD Venice and Intel Oak Stream CPU) continues to
face delays, and we believe the delays are partly due to potential shortages of key
components across the general server supply chain (including ABF substrate, memory,
PCB/CCL, etc), (2) growing contribution from margin-dilutive products starting from

2H26 (including QD and SOCAMM, which accounted for 4-5% of total 3Q26 revenue per
GSe), and (3) rising material cost could weigh on profitability as the company has limited
pricing power to fully pass on cost increases without risking market share.
However, we remain constructive on the outlook for general servers (we now expect
general server volume to increase by 20%/12%/8% YoY in 2026E-28E, was 14%/6%/6%
previously, see here), which we believe Lotes is the key beneficiary with its solid market

share in the server CPU socket market. We expect Lotes’ server revenue to see 38%
2026-28E CAGR (tracking above the 28% CAGR seen during the previous high-growth
server cycle in 2019-22), and we continue to believe server is the key margin expansion
driver given its above corporate average margin (general server socket GM at ~60% vs.
3Q26 total GM of 45%) and >55% revenue contribution throughout 2026E-28E.
Overall, we believe Lotes’ current share price has largely priced in the positive outlook

for general servers (the stock is trading at 16x+ 2027E P/E based on GSe/BBG
consensus, which is +2 STDV above the average P/E during the previous high growth
server cycle in 2019-22). The revenue contribution from higher margin new products
(including next gen CPU sockets, GPU sockets, and NPO products) is likely to remain
limited in the near-term.
We maintain our Neutral rating and lower our 12m TP to NT$1,640 (from NT1,670).
3Q26 overview
Lotes’ 3Q26 core business (OPI) was 23%/15% lower than GSe and BBG consensus, with

GM 1.6ppt/1.0ppt lower than GSe/BBG consensus, which was driven by higher material
costs and higher contribution from new AI-related products with lower margins. The
OPEX to sales ratio was 2.7ppt/2.4ppt higher than GSe and BBG consensus, due to
increasing R&D spending and labor costs. For earnings, Lotes’ NI was 18%/15% lower
14 November 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Lotes (3533.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

than GSe/BBG consensus, mainly driven by the lower than expected GM.

Earnings revisions: 
We revise down our 2026E revenue by 5% to factor in 3Q26 actual results and revise 
down our 2027E revenue by 1% to factor in delay in new CPU socket products, but revise 
up our 2028E/2029E revenue estimates by 2%/2% to factor in the 
better-than-expected contribution from the server business. We revise down our 
2026/27/28/29E GM estimates by 1.2/2.2/1.2/0.9ppt to factor in the ongoing rising 
material costs and delay in new CPU socket products. Overall, we revise down our 
2026/27/28E earnings estimates by 11/8/1%. 
Valuation:  
Maintain Neutral rating and revise down our 12-m TP to NT$1,640 (from NT$1,670), 
based on an unchanged P/E of 16.0x (which is at the company’s average valuation since 
2020) and applied to 2027E EPS (rolled over from 4Q26-3Q27E), and factor in our 
earnings revisions.

### Page 5

Lotes is a connector supplier mainly focusing on the niche CPU socket market with more 
than 40% share in 2024, but we see pressure for its market share outlook in the coming 
years considering the intensifying competition in the server CPU market. We maintain 
our positive view on the company’s GM expansion potential longer term, which we 
believe will be mainly driven by better product mix (Venice server CPU to be released in 
2H26 and Oak Stream server CPU to be released in 2027, both with better ASP and 
margin). Meanwhile, Lotes’ CPU socket product line’s ASP/GM are higher than the 
company average, and we see scope for both to grow even faster in the new product 
generation given the larger socket size and pin count required. However, the company’s 
new product line, Quick Disconnects (UQD or MQD) for liquid cooling systems, is likely to 
deliver very limited revenue/gross profit contribution in the coming years (0.6/1.2% 
revenue and 0.4/0.8% GP contribution in 2026E/2027E) given thermal solution 
providers typically ship cold plates as integrated modules and leading thermal suppliers 
tend to secure QD supply in-house or through affiliated suppliers. We are Neutral rated 
on Lotes considering the share loss risk in server sockets and the shares trading at the 
historical peak P/E level, making valuation less attractive, despite the company’s solid 
revenue/GM expansion trend driven by socket upgrading demand. Key risks: (1) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected server shipment growth which would raise/lower 
server CPU socket demand; (2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs without 
product pricing adjustment; (3) slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related 
contribution.

Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,640 is based on a target 
P/E of 16.0x (in line with the company’s average valuation since 2020) applied to our 
2027E EPS.  
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Stronger-/weaker-than-expected server CPU demand, 
(2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs, and (3) 
slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related contribution.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Potential to win next gen TPU projects, while other ASIC
customers remain undisclosed.
1. Major AI ASIC project (TPU) - potential to win next generation projects.
• TPU v8 shipment track, US$2.5bn revenue in 4Q26. Reiterated US$16bn
revenue in 2027, high end of guidance. As there are more customers than
chip supply, if ABF substrate supply improves, revenue can improve.
• TPU v9 EMIB-T substrate supply is also key, company is comfortable on yield

and output trajectory.
• It sees its TPU v9 project as a major TPU SKUs for the customer. MediaTek
revenue per chip is higher than it was in v8. The compute die is handled by
the customer.
• MediaTek said it is possible for the customer to use designed TPU (eg. v8t
and v9) for AI LLM customer's rack system, besides Google's internal use.
• On third generation (TPU v10), MediaTek is working with customer's internal
team, and has high confidence to win the project.

• New generation TPU compute die handled by customer. MediaTek will focus
on D2D inference, I/O die, and chip packaging technology.
• We maintain our view that Alchip is strong candidate for TPU compute die
backend design. Based on Alchip’s NRE revenue timing, we think the
compute die could be for the TPU v10.
2. Other ASIC customers - awaiting more disclosure: MediaTek still cannot
disclose the non-China CSP customer name or project details. The engagement with

China CSP is ongoing, but needs to comply with US BIS rules.
3. ASIC business financial profile - OP margin improvement: Mediatek said a major
customer wanted to drive to full COT (customer own tool), but with the chip's
complexity growing, it hopes to sustain ASIC margin in future generations. The
company reiterated its 3Q earning call comments that ASIC op. margin is 10% above
the previous average. It reiterated its op margin can improve to 20%+ in 2027 on

higher ASIC revenue contribution. We estimate ASIC op. margin at 25-30%.
4. Smartphone business - still challenging: High memory costs continue to impact
China demand. Blended ASP for MediaTek's SoC has risen on price hikes and better
product mix.
Reiterate OW & Top Pick: MediaTek continues to win new TPU projects with higher
revenue per chip, and so revenue growth may extend to 2030-31. The chance to
supply to AI LLM for end-customer's rack system via a major customer could also

the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 169: 3324雙鴻 台新

Source PDF: all report pdf/3324雙鴻 台新.pdf

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

雙鴻(3324)：3Q26 財報不如預期，營運逐季向上 
建議：維持買進

一、雙鴻為散熱解決方案供應商  雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，為國內前二大 NB 散熱模組廠，過去主要生產散熱
器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，現在已轉型為提供客戶完整散熱解
決方案供應商。主要應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、智慧型手機及伺服器等領
域。3Q26 產品應用領域 Server 81%、PC 14%、VGA/Gaming 4%、及 Others(手
機及車用) 1%。另外 3Q26 水冷業務比重 62%(1H26 約為 55~60%)。

二、3Q26 毛利率 25.65%，低於
預期，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元 
 雙鴻 3Q26 EPS 11.59 元，低於台新預估的 14.13 元，獲利達成率 82%。本業
來看，3Q26 營收 97.87 億元，+12.5%QOQ，+64.3%YOY，低於市場預期之
+15~20%QOQ，七月營收有遞延認列，實際拉貨力道未如預期顯著，3Q26 毛
利率 25.65%，較 2Q26 再衰退 2.77 個百分點，亦低於台新預估的 30%，主要
是報廢品打消，預期 4Q26 毛利率回升，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元。雙鴻 10 月營收
33.72 億元，+17%MOM，+45%YOY，獲利達成率 31%，符合預期，預期 11 月
營收及 12 月營收將優於 10 月，預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，+12.8%QOQ，
+45.6%YOY，預估 4Q26 毛利率 28%，預估 4Q26 EPS 14.79 元。

三、營運逐季成長，氣冷及水冷
兩塊業務均樂觀 
 2025 年雙鴻水冷營收比重 35%，1Q26 提升至 55%，3Q26 提升至 62%，預期
2027 年將提升至 80%，帶動公司業績大幅增長。氣冷部分，2024 年月營收 2-
2.5 億元，2025 年 4～4.5 億元，2026 年平均 6～7 億元，氣冷水冷均樂觀，以
AWS TR3 來看，氣冷及水冷比重至少是 70~80%對比 20~30%，TR4 將為 100%
全水冷，水冷大趨勢不變，VR 9 月起量，VR 營收逐季增，TR3 9 月起量 Compute 
tray 及 Switch tray 及 Rack manifolds 都有相關產品出貨，預計 12 月單月 ASIC
營收比重有機會來到 15~25%，ASIC 水冷產值高於GPU 客戶，其中 Mata>AWS，
2027 年 ASIC 增長可期，公司預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU。2026 年可
視為 CDU 元年，2027 年營收跳增可期。台新預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，
BVPS 11/18 收盤價 2027 PER 建議

+10~15%QOQ，2026 年營收+60~70%YOY，4Q26 毛利率 28%，2027 年營收

高資本支出。

四、維持買進理由  3Q26 毛利率仍不如預期，故下修未來各季毛利率，氣冷及水冷兩塊業務樂觀，

預估 2027 年 EPS 76.62 元(原估 90.36 元)，目標價 1200 元。

公司簡介 
上品為從事氟素樹脂材料相關製品之生產
廠商，主要經營氟素樹脂材料（鐵氟龍）相
關製品，包括氟素原料加工之半成品及成品
生產及銷售，下游應用廣泛，是全球氟素樹
脂加工應用廠商中少數能同時滿足各階段
設備、製造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務
的企業。3Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設
備營收占 69.7% ， 氟 素 內 襯 管 件 類 占
10.2%，氟素應用材料類占 16.5 %，氟素
工程收入占 3.7%。3Q26 公司營收按地區
別區分，台灣占 47.4%，中國占 48.2%，美
國占 0.5%，其他亞洲占 1.6%，歐洲占
2.2%。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

上品 (4770 TT) 
在手訂單金額提升，持續擴充產能因應

永豐觀點 
上品在手訂單金額提升至 80 億元，隨著先前低毛利訂單陸續消化完畢，產品組合
預期改善，公司規劃於 2027 持續進行產能擴充，後市展望持續正向，研究部維持
正向看待上品獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進，主要係因：上品在手訂單金額持續提升至 80 億元，反映台灣
及中國地區接單表現持續暢旺，隨著公司低毛利訂單出貨完畢，產品組合應逐步好
轉，且公司規劃於 2027 持續擴充產能，挹注未來營收動能，研究部預估公司
2026/2027 稅後 EPS 14.55/23.01 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位於 10-20X，以
2027 年預估 EPS，給予 15X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，投資建議維持買進。

ESG 評析 
上品企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

上品為從事氟素樹脂材料相關製品之生產廠商：公司成立於 1981 年，主要經營氟
素樹脂材料（鐵氟龍）相關製品，包括氟素原料加工之半成品及成品生產及銷售，
下游應用廣泛，是全球氟素樹脂加工應用廠商中少數能同時滿足各階段設備、製
造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務的企業。3Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設備營
收占 69.7%，氟素內襯管件類占 10.2%，氟素應用材料類占 16.5 %，氟素工程收
入占 3.7%；3Q26 公司產品依應用類別區分，半導體占營收 95.1%，化工應用占
比為 3.1%，面板及太陽能應用占 1.4%，其他應用占 0.4%，3Q26 公司營收按地
區別區分，台灣占 47.4%，中國占 48.2%，美國占 0.5%，其他亞洲占 1.6%，歐
洲占 2.2%。 
3Q26 EPS 4.27 元，優於預期：上品 3Q26 營收 16 億元（+22.5% QoQ，+14.4% 
YoY），優於預期，主因上品於 2Q26 擴產，並持續拉升產能利用率，推升營收表
現，毛利率 34.64%（-1.91pts. QoQ，+4.91pts. YoY），低於預期，主因上品出
貨較多低毛利訂單，影響產品組合所致，稅後淨利 34.1 億元（+37.2% QoQ，
+39.9% YoY），優於預期，其中，營業費用低於預期，主因認列預期信用損失金
額降低所致，3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.27 元，優於預期。

### Page 3

3

在手訂單金額持續提升：上品在手訂單金額達 80 億元，較前次發報告時 75 億元
持續提升，其中，約 75%為彰濱廠訂單，約 25%為嘉興廠訂單，預期到年底在手
訂單金額將持續再提升。2026 接單槽車數量達 1,100 台，反映台灣化學品廠因應
晶圓廠大客戶需求而擴充產能，對於上品內襯設備及內襯管件需求同步增溫，而中
國記憶體廠及化學品廠亦持續擴充，下單動能有所增溫。隨著市況於 2026 大幅改
善，彰濱廠及嘉興廠已可挑選毛利率較佳的訂單進行承接，產品組合預期將逐步好
轉，有利其毛利率逐步回升。 
2027 上品計畫持續擴充產能：因應客戶需求強勁，公司於 2Q26 進行擴產，並持
續招工補充人力，拉升產能利用率，目前彰濱廠及嘉興廠已達近滿載水準，公司年
初彰濱廠槽車約當產能約為月產能 80 台，近期已提升至月產能 130 台，預期年底
將達滿載規模月產能 140 台，公司規劃將於 6M27 再擴充至月產能 180 台，嘉興
廠亦規劃提升產能約 30%，以因應客戶強勁需求。而先前承接的低毛利訂單仍有
部分尚未出貨完畢，預期將於 1Q27 出貨完畢，因營收比重持續下降，預期產品組
合應持續改善，有利毛利率及獲利表現回升。

後市展望正向，投資建議維持買進：展望後市，台灣化學品廠及中國記憶體、化學
品廠擴建，推升對上品氟素樹脂產品需求，上品在手訂單金額持續提升，隨著上品
先前承接低毛利訂單陸續出貨完畢，產品組合應逐步改善，推升毛利率及獲利表現
回升。上品規劃於 2027 持續進行產能擴充，挹注未來營收動能，且已可挑選毛利
率較佳的訂單進行承接，後市獲利展望正向。研究部預估公司 2026 營收 56.9 億
元（+20.3% YoY），毛利率 35.1%，稅後淨利 11.6 億元（+41.6% YoY），稅後
EPS 14.55 元，預估 2027 營收 76.8 億元（+35.1% YoY），毛利率 38.31%，稅
後淨利 18.4 億元（+59.2% YoY），稅後 EPS 23.01 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位
於 10-20X，以 2027 年預估 EPS，給予 15X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，維持買進
投資建議。

## Document 226: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업

 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

## Document 234: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0.pdf

Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q 부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520 억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1 조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV 에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

## Document 256: AI in 2027_ 20 questions for the global economy

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI in 2027_ 20 questions for the global economy.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 24
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The AI boom is reshaping growth, productivity, trade, jobs and
markets. Investment still has room to run in 2027, but
ﬁnancing is getting riskier and expectations are sky-high. We
ask how long it can last, who stands to gain and what happens
if the promised returns fail to arrive
Carsten Brzeski, Bert Colijn, James Knightley, Deepali Bhargava,
James Smith, Lynn Song, Jan Frederik Slijkerman and Amrita Naik
Nimbalkar
In a nutshell
The AI boom still has room to run. Investment is likely to

continue rising in 2027, supported by strong demand
and constrained supply. But its contribution to US growth
is probably close to its peak as the sheer scale of
investment makes further acceleration harder.
The boom is entering a new phase. The story is
expanding beyond chips and data centres into power
generation, grids and cooling. At the same time,
ﬁnancing is shifting gradually from hyperscalers'
enormous cash ﬂows towards debt, private credit and

### Page 2

more complex structures, increasing the risks if expected
returns fail to materialise.
The economic eﬀects are becoming broader and more
complicated. AI is supporting US growth and global
technology supply chains, but may also be diverting
investment from other parts of the economy. It is adding
to inﬂationary pressures today through demand for
energy, equipment and skilled labour, while the hoped-
for productivity and disinﬂation beneﬁts are likely to take

longer to emerge.
The winners will not necessarily be those building the
most AI infrastructure. Europe and Asia are beneﬁting
from critical positions in the supply chain, while
ultimately the largest gains may go to companies and
economies that use AI most eﬀectively.
Our base case is a plateau, not a bust. Today's leading
technology companies are highly proﬁtable and much
less leveraged than their dotcom predecessors. But
expectations are exceptionally high. The crucial question

for 2027 is whether AI revenues and productivity gains
begin to justify the extraordinary investment. If they do
not, overcapacity, tighter ﬁnancing and falling valuations
could turn a plateau into a much harder landing.
How big, how long?
Railroads, electriﬁcation, dotcom ﬁbre: how
does today's AI boom compare with the
great investment manias of the past?
Bigger than the dotcom boom, more important for growth and, so
far, more safely ﬁnanced.
Investment in US data centre structures, computers and software

reached 5.6% of GDP in the second quarter of 2026, up from 3.8% in
2022, or 5.2% once imported hardware is netted out. That already

### Page 3

puts the current boom above the dotcom peak in 2000, when
business investment in computers, communications equipment
and software reached about 4.5% of GDP, although still below
Britain’s railway mania of the 1840s, when railway investment
alone reached roughly 6-7% of GDP.
So while this is not the biggest investment boom in history, it is
already bigger than the last great technology boom. And it is
having a much bigger economic impact: IT investment accounted

for 16-18% of US GDP growth in 1998-2000; AI-related investment
now accounts for 37%.
The biggest diﬀerence though is how this boom is being ﬁnanced.
Railways and telecom networks were built on debt and ended in
defaults, largely through unrated railroad bonds in the 19th century
and high-yield telecom debt after 2000. This time, investment has
so far been funded mainly from the cash ﬂows of some of the
world’s most proﬁtable companies, with debt and equity only now

being added at the margin.
The assets are diﬀerent, too. Rails and ﬁbre lasted for decades. Data
centre shells, grids and power plants will also have long lives, but
GPUs may have an economic life of just three to six years. That
much shorter shelf life is central to the debate over whether today’s
investment will generate suﬃcient returns.
Still, the payoﬀ could come faster. Factories took decades to
redesign their ﬂoors around the electric motor. AI coding tools are

paying oﬀ within months, and adoption is already disrupting
software, accounting, online retail and advertising.
The common thread is that transformative technology does not
necessarily mean good returns for those who ﬁnance the
transformation. The Nasdaq lost almost 80% between 2000 and
2002, yet the ﬁbre laid during the boom went on to underpin the
streaming economy. Investors build the road; others get to drive on
it.
How much more will be invested in AI in

2027, and is the boom still accelerating?

### Page 4

Still accelerating in dollars, but probably peaking in its
contribution to growth.
Momentum remains strong. Taiwan's leading semiconductor
companies report order books that are full on a rolling 18-month
basis, with any new capacity snapped up as soon as it comes
online. Industry forecasts point to tight supply-demand conditions
until 2028. Barring a wave of order cancellations or defaults, that
points to another year of heavy AI investment in 2027, with

hyperscaler capex expected to exceed US$1 trillion, increasingly
geared towards AI infrastructure.
So is the boom peaking? In dollar terms, no. The constraint today is
not appetite but supply: chips, power, permits and skilled
electricians. When the bottleneck is the factory gate rather than the
order book, spending is more likely to be stretched out than cut
back.
But economists should be careful what they wish for. GDP growth
responds to the change in investment, not its level. Investment that

rises 50% a year adds a lot to growth; investment that rises 10%
from a much bigger base adds far less; and investment that merely
holds steady at a record high adds nothing at all. As the base gets
bigger, the arithmetic gets tougher. That is why we expect AI
investment to keep rising in 2027 while its contribution to US
growth should peak.
What would signal a genuine top? Shortening lead times for chips
and equipment, cancelled or postponed data centre projects, capex

guidance that stops being revised up, and a widening gap between
investment and AI revenues.
For now, the bigger risk is not that the money runs out in 2027, but
that it keeps ﬂowing so fast that the questions about returns grow
much louder in 2028.
Chips and data centres are only the ﬁrst
layer. How big is the second wave of

### Page 5

investment in grids, power generation and
cooling?
Bigger, slower and longer-lived than the ﬁrst wave – and
potentially more important for the macro outlook.
As a rule of thumb, US$1bn buys roughly 25MW of fully equipped
AI compute capacity. Not all of the c.US$1 trillion of hyperscaler
capex expected in 2027 will go to AI compute; some will fund
conventional cloud infrastructure. But even if only half does, that
implies more than 12GW of additional power demand – roughly the

output of a dozen large nuclear reactors, needed around the clock.
That is where the second wave comes in: transmission lines,
substations and transformers, gas turbines and renewables,
battery storage and industrial cooling. In the US, grid connection
queues and transformer lead times have already become binding
constraints, and some developers are building their own generation
on site rather than waiting for the grid.
This second wave diﬀers from the ﬁrst in three ways. First, the

assets last for decades rather than years. A GPU may be obsolete
by 2030; a substation built today will still be humming in 2060.
That makes grid investment less vulnerable to a sudden AI
downturn and more like the railway build-out of the 19th century.
Second, it is slower. Permitting, planning and construction take
years, which spreads the investment impulse over time and could
stretch out the cycle. Third, it is more widely shared: grid and power

equipment draws on suppliers in Europe and Asia as well as US
construction and engineering ﬁrms, so the beneﬁts travel further.
There is a catch. Somebody has to pay for the grid, and in
regulated markets that somebody is often the household electricity
customer. Rising power bills are already turning data centres into a
political issue in parts of the US, and they feed straight into the
inﬂation debate.
Chips are the sprint. Power is the marathon – and the marathon

may end up mattering more.

### Page 6

Is this an American party, or does Europe
get an invitation? Who supplies the picks
and shovels?
So far it is very much an American party – but Europe supplies the
catering, and may turn out to be the smarter guest.
The investment gap is stark. US imports of computer equipment
and semiconductors, a rough proxy for AI investment, have risen by
about 258% since 2019, compared with 38% in the UK. Europe's
own build-out is held back by high energy prices, scarce grid

capacity – think of the data centre moratoria in Dublin and
Amsterdam – and fragmented capital markets. The EU's AI
gigafactories initiative is an attempt to catch up.
But Europe sells crucial picks and shovels. ASML expects €44-45bn
of sales in 2026 and says customers keep raising their equipment-
demand forecasts because of the scale and duration of AI
investment. Inﬁneon expects its AI-related revenues to rise from
around €1.5bn in FY2026 to €2.5bn in FY2027. Add electrical and

grid equipment, cables, power generation and cooling, and
Europe's industrial base is well plugged into the American build-
out.
Asia has been one of the biggest beneﬁciaries of the AI boom. The
gains extend well beyond Korea and China, which have beneﬁted
from surging demand for memory chips and advanced technology
hardware. Across ASEAN, economies have also attracted signiﬁcant
FDI into semiconductors, electronics manufacturing, and
supporting supply chains as ﬁrms expand capacity and diversify

production networks.
More importantly, the broad economic gains from AI come from
using it, not building it – and here Europe is surprisingly good.
Uptake is strong, and European SMEs use AI almost as intensively
as large corporates, because the upfront investment is low. Nor
does AI look like a winner-takes-all technology: switching costs are
low and network eﬀects limited. If model providers end up
competing on price, the spoils go to the users – and Europe does

### Page 7

not need to build nearly as many data centres to compete. Late to
the construction site, Europe could still be early to move in.
There is a silver lining, too. Europe is far less exposed if the boom
turns to bust. Its AI capex is smaller and the wealth channel much
weaker: equities make up 13% of UK households' ﬁnancial assets,
versus 44% in the US. Missing the party also means skipping the
hangover.
Which sectors stand to win most, and which
risk being left behind?

The winners sell the infrastructure or use AI to do more; the losers
are those whose business model is the very task AI now performs.
The clearest winners are the suppliers of the build-out:
semiconductors and chip equipment, utilities and grid operators,
electrical equipment, construction, cooling and data centre real
estate. They are already seeing it in their order books.
Software is mixed. AI is a powerful selling point, but it also
threatens the per-seat pricing model: if one employee with an AI

agent does the work of ﬁve, the customer needs fewer licences.
Most at risk are legacy IT services and business process
outsourcing, call centres, parts of professional services, and media
and advertising models that depend on search traﬃc. AI-based
services may also disrupt online retail, including online travel
agencies, as AI assistants increasingly do the browsing and the
booking.
But don't write oﬀ every exposed sector. The Jevons paradox –
cheaper supply raising demand – could favour some of them. Legal

services is a good example: if AI makes contract review or legal
advice much cheaper, demand may rise faster than the cost per
task falls. Growth may also come from unexpected places. In
healthcare, AI-driven drug discovery and diagnostics could open up
new treatments, creating demand rather than merely cutting costs.
Ultimately, productivity gains depend more on the tasks
employees perform than on the sector they sit in. Workers handling

the faster AI can travel through it.
In short, the winners will be those that build AI or use it to work
smarter. The losers will be those whose core activities can
increasingly be automated.

Growth, prices and trade
How much of US growth in 2027 will come
from AI capex, and what would growth look
like without it?
Today, more than a third. In 2027, probably less – which is exactly
why the rest of the economy matters.
At ﬁrst glance, AI looks like the engine of the entire US economy.
Data centre construction, equipment investment and software
spending have grown at a pace that makes their headline
contribution to GDP growth look spectacular. But much of that

money leaves the country straight away: from Taiwanese chips to
servers assembled abroad, the hardware is largely imported.
Netting out those imports, we estimate that AI-related investment
accounts for 37% of US real GDP growth. Substantial, but a long
way from the 'AI is the only game in town' narrative.
The more uncomfortable ﬁnding lies outside tech. Rather than
lifting the overall growth rate, the AI boom seems to have
cannibalised growth elsewhere. Strip out AI, and the rest of US

private non-residential investment had fallen year-on-year for six
consecutive quarters by mid-2026. Before 2021, tech and non-tech
investment moved broadly in step; since then, they have moved in
opposite directions. One explanation: shareholders reward
companies that embrace AI, so capital budgets are being redirected
towards tech at the expense of everything else. So the boom isn't

### Page 9

so much about creating additional investment as redirecting
investment from elsewhere in the economy.
What would US growth look like without AI? Not simply 37% lower.
Part of the capital, engineers and electricity absorbed by AI would
have found other uses. A world without the AI boom would most
likely show weaker growth, but not by the full amount of AI's
contribution – and with a more balanced investment mix.
For 2027, our plateau base case implies a smaller AI contribution to

growth. That would give the non-tech economy room to breathe –
but only if it is ready to take over.
For now, AI is a capex story, not a consumer story. And capex
stories have a habit of ending with a hangover.
The productivity question: when, if ever,
does AI show up in the statistics?
In the US and China, it already does – partly. The big gains are still
to come, and may arrive abruptly.
It depends on where you look. In the US, labour productivity has

picked up markedly since the start of the AI boom. Part of that
comes from high-skilled services, exactly where you would expect
AI to show up ﬁrst. But part of it reﬂects the enormous data centre
investments, which are capital-heavy and need very little labour.
Total factor productivity – the best available measure of gains from
technological progress – has improved decently in the US, but
without an exceptional acceleration. While AI-related sectors are

providing a signiﬁcant boost to several Asian economies, this
largely reﬂects rapid investment and higher capital utilisation
rather than a sustained improvement in underlying productivity.
However, in China, we’ve seen labour productivity growth pick up in
the last two years, breaking what had been an extended downtrend
as Chinese companies race to incorporate AI in workﬂows. In the
eurozone, which lacks comparable AI investment, labour
productivity growth remains lacklustre, with very few exceptions,

like for example, in the Netherlands.

### Page 10

The technology holds real promise for broad-based productivity
gains. But reaping them requires wider adoption and investment in
infrastructure and skills, both within companies and across the
economy. That suggests a J-curve: heavy upfront intangible
investment – time spent learning new skills and reorganising
workﬂows – could initially mute measured productivity before the
investment pays oﬀ, possibly quite abruptly. Electriﬁcation is the
classic precedent: the electric motor arrived in the 1880s, but

factory productivity only took oﬀ in the 1920s, once factories had
been rebuilt around it.
And even then, a shift of the workforce towards sectors where
productivity gains are harder to achieve – think healthcare and care
work – would dampen the macroeconomic eﬀect. Measurement
doesn't help either: when AI makes a free digital service better, GDP
statistics barely notice.
Still, after decades of slowing productivity growth, AI is likely to be a
game changer. The question is not so much whether it will show up

in the statistics, but when. In 1987, Robert Solow quipped that you
could see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity
statistics. It took about a decade to prove him wrong.
Is AI inﬂationary or deﬂationary?
Electricity, memory chips and construction
costs point one way; long-run efﬁciency
gains point the other.
Inﬂationary today, disinﬂationary tomorrow – but don't hold your
breath for the outright deﬂation Silicon Valley promises.
It depends on your time horizon. For now, the eﬀect runs in the

inﬂationary direction. AI's enormous energy demand is adding to
the current energy shock, memory chip prices have surged, and the
scramble for electricians, transformers and construction capacity is
pushing up costs. Meanwhile, the investment boom is pouring fuel
on the ﬁre of a US economy already running with above-target
inﬂation.

### Page 11

In the longer run, once the technology lifts productivity, it should
lower unit labour costs and exert disinﬂationary pressure across the
economy. Like most technological breakthroughs, AI will probably
end up being disinﬂationary. But the eﬀect will be concentrated in
services and much smaller for goods with physical inputs.
Algorithms can't make steel, copper or concrete cheaper.
There is also a less comfortable twist. AI allows companies to price

dynamically, adjusting prices to what each customer is willing to
pay and thereby capturing more of the consumer surplus. That
could make some prices stickier rather than lower. Together with
the physical constraints, this makes the outright deﬂation Sam
Altman envisages rather unlikely.
For AI itself, the picture is diﬀerent. As we argued in 2024, we expect
a downward price trend for AI services, as with other software
categories, and at the sector level we continue to hold that view.

Competition between model providers should keep the pressure on.
For central banks, this means the AI disinﬂation dividend is a story
for the second half of the decade, not for 2027. Until then, the
boom adds to demand faster than it adds to supply – the textbook
recipe for inﬂation, not deﬂation.
How is AI reshaping global trade and
investment ﬂows, from Taiwanese chips to
Dutch lithography?
AI is redrawing global trade around a single supply chain – and

rewarding the countries that own its choke points.
AI is increasingly sorting the global economy into winners and
laggards. The IMF has raised its outlook for economies beneﬁting
from the AI investment boom, particularly those embedded in the
global technology supply chain, while countries outside it see
much less of a boost. Taiwan is riding booming semiconductor
demand, South Korea is beneﬁting from demand for high-
bandwidth memory, and the eﬀects travel upstream to the

suppliers of advanced chipmaking equipment, including the
Netherlands.

### Page 12

This illustrates how AI is reshaping both trade and investment
ﬂows. Spending on data centres and computing capacity in the US
drives demand for chips from Asia, which in turn requires
investment in semiconductor capacity and equipment from Europe,
Japan and the US. The result is an international AI investment
chain, with growth increasingly ﬂowing to the countries that hold
its critical links. It also explains a good part of the surge in the US
import bill: every data centre built in Texas shows up in Taiwan's

export statistics.
Trade policy is the second force reshaping the chain. Export controls
on advanced chips to China, tariﬀs and subsidy programmes such
as the US Chips Act (or direct government stakes in companies) are
pushing companies to build capacity closer to home or in friendly
countries. That makes the chain more resilient on paper, but also
more expensive – another reason why the deﬂationary promise of
AI may take longer to materialise.
The ﬂip side is concentration risk. If the chain snaps – through a

demand slump, an escalation of export controls or a Taiwan crisis –
the countries beneﬁting most today would feel it ﬁrst and hardest.
What does AI mean for labour markets, and
for how central banks read them?
So far, AI is reshaping who gets hired rather than how many are
employed – which makes it a real-time headache for central
banks.
Evidence on AI's labour market impact remains fragmented, and
the economy-wide eﬀect is still limited. If history is any guide, fears

of mass unemployment are overdone: with every major technology
of past centuries, growth was strong enough to create jobs
elsewhere, even as demand for some types of labour fell. But if
adoption is so fast that labour demand and supply can't adjust in
time, unemployment could rise temporarily.
The ﬁrst cracks are showing at the entry level. In the US, job growth
has remained positive but weaker in sectors with high AI exposure;
evidence from the eurozone is mixed. The Dallas Fed ﬁnds that

### Page 13

employment among young workers in AI-exposed occupations has
weakened, mainly reﬂecting lower hiring rather than more layoﬀs.
According to the Stanford Digital Economy Lab, employment
among 22-25-year-olds in highly exposed occupations was around
19% below where it would have been by June 2026 had it kept
pace with less-exposed occupations. In China, we see similar
pressures on entry-level work reﬂected in rising youth
unemployment. The Chinese government has planned training and

job replacement policies to help alleviate any negative eﬀects of AI
on employment. For developing Asia, particularly India and the
Philippines where outsourcing sectors are highly exposed to AI, the
evidence points to adaptation rather than decline. AI is changing
the nature of outsourced work, not reducing demand for it.
AI is not ﬁring people; it is quietly not hiring them. Still, none of this
points to mass displacement. Yet. And with ageing populations and

persistent labour shortages, the real question may be whether
shortages or AI turn out to be the bigger problem.
For central banks, AI is less of a short-run issue than you might
expect. Higher productivity growth combined with more muted job
growth could be the healthy sign of an economy absorbing a new
technology, and come with more moderate inﬂation. The tricky
part is reading it in real time. A soft payrolls print could signal either
a weakening economy or a more productive one – and the right

policy response would be very diﬀerent. Get it wrong, and a central
bank either cuts rates into a productivity boom or sits on its hands
as the economy weakens.
Who pays?
Is the boom still funded from cash ﬂows, or
are hyperscalers increasingly turning to
debt?
Still mostly from cash ﬂows – but the cushion is thinning and the
borrowing has begun.

### Page 14

The largest hyperscalers have so far paid for the AI build-out out of
their own pockets, which is the biggest diﬀerence from the dotcom
and railway booms. But capex is absorbing a rising share of
operating cash ﬂow, squeezing free cash ﬂow and leaving less room
for share buybacks. For companies that used to return cash to
shareholders at a record pace, that is a notable shift in priorities.
Borrowing is stepping up. Several hyperscalers, such as Amazon,

Alphabet, Meta, and Oracle have issued jumbo bond deals since
2025, with Oracle the most leveraged of the group. Equity markets
are being tapped too: several big tech ﬁrms announced plans to
raise equity in June. None of this is alarming on its own – these are
investment-grade companies with strong balance sheets – but it
marks a change of phase. Cash-funded booms are self-limiting:
they stop when the cash runs out. Debt-funded booms can run
further, and fall harder.

The key is to distinguish between the players. The cash-rich
hyperscalers can absorb a disappointment in AI returns; their core
businesses in advertising, cloud and software keep generating cash
regardless. The neoclouds and AI labs are a diﬀerent story. They
depend entirely on outside money – venture capital, private credit,
vendor ﬁnancing and strategic investors – and their business
models only work if revenue keeps growing spectacularly. They are

the most likely point of stress if ﬁnancing conditions tighten.
In between sits a growing grey zone of leases, joint ventures and
special purpose vehicles that keep debt oﬀ the hyperscalers' own
books.
The direction of travel is clear. The boom started as a self-funded
venture by the richest companies in the world. It is gradually
turning into a credit story.
Leases, SPVs, private credit: how opaque
has AI ﬁnancing become?
More opaque than the hyperscalers' balance sheets suggest – but

so far, the risk rests on some of the strongest borrowers in the
world.
